4a1(1): / /la zhum ba myed pa dang /snyand pa myi 'dod pa dang /myi snyand pa la khong khro ba myed pa dang /bstod pa la dga' ba myed pa dang /smad pa la myi dga' ba myed pa dang /bde ba la mngon bar ma chags pa dang /sdug bsngal la yid byung bar myi byed pa dang /
4a2(1): 'du byed rnams lend par myi byed pa dang /bsngags pa yang dag pa la ma chags pa dang /bsngags pa yangadag pa ma yin ba la myi mjed pa dang /khyim pa dang rab tu byung ba dag dang myi bstend pa dang /spyod yul ma yin ba rnam par spong ba dang /spyod yul du rgyu ba dang /
4a3(1): spyod pa phun sum tshogs pa dang /spyod pa ma yin ba rnam par spongab dang /rigs sun par myi byed pa dang /bstand pa srung ba dang /smra ba nyung zhing mnyend pa dang /dal gyis smra ba dang /tshig gi lan glan ba la mkhas pa dang /phyir rgol ba tshar gcod pa dang /dus su 
4a4(1): 'gro bar byed pa dang /so so'i skye bo la yid myi rtond pa dang /sdug bsngal ba rnams la khyad du gsod par myi byed cing de dag la nor sbyin pa dang /dbul po rnams la brnyas par myi byed pa dang /tshul khrims 'chald pa la g.yo myed pas stend pa dang /bdog pa thams cad yongs su gtong ba 
4a5(1): blo dang /chos kyis phan 'dogs pa dang /zang zing yongs su gtong ba dang /myi stsogs par gnas pa dang /tshul khrims la stod cing 'chald pa'i tshul khrims la smod pa dang /tshul khrims dang ldan ba la g.yo myed pas stend par dang /bdog pa thams can yongs su gtong ba 
4a6(1): dang /lhag pa'i bsam bas gnyer ba dang /ci skad smras pa bzhin byed pa dang /rgyun myi 'chad par sbyor ba dang /gus par byas te dga' ba myong bar byed pa dang /dpe' shes pa dang /sngon byung ba la mkhas pa dang /dge' ba'i rtsa ba sngond tu 'gro ba dang /thabs la mkhas pa dang /
4a7(1): mtshan ma spong ba dang /'du shes zlog pa dang /dngos po rnams yongs su shes pa dang /mdo sde mngon bar sgrub pa dang /'dul ba la mkhas pa dang /bden ba gtan la 'bebs pa dang /rnam par grol ba mngon tu bya ba la 'jug pa dang /tshig mgo gcig tu smra ba dang /ci lta ba 
4a8(1): bzhin du shes shing mthong ba myi gtong ba dang /nem nur myed pa'i tshig dang /stong pa nyid la rtend pa dang /mtshan ma myed pa la rtend pa dang /smon pa myed pa'i ngo bo nyid rtogs par byed pa dang /myi 'jigs pa thob pa dang /ye shes kyis snang bar byed pa dang /tshul khrims kyi dam pa 
4a9(1): dang /snyoms par 'jug pa la gzhol ba dang /gcig pur dga' ba dang /bdag shes pa dang /shes kyi khe nyu dus chog shes pa dang /sems la rnyog pa myed pa dang /lta bar gyurd pa rnam par spong ba dang /gzungs thob pa dang /ye shes la 'jug pa dang /gnas dang gnas ma 
4a10(1): yin ba dang /'jug pa dang /bsgrub pa shes pa dang /gtan tshigs dang /rig pa dang /tshul dang /sgo dang /rgyu dang /lam dang /nan tan dang /bsgrub pa dang /phrin dang /gdams pa dang /bstand pa la spyod pa dang /rjes su 'thun ba'i bzod pa dang /bzod pa'i sa dang /myi 
4a11(1): bzod pa dang bral ba dang /ye shes kyi sa dang /myi shes pa spong ba dang /shes pa la gnas pa dang /rnal 'byord spyod pa'i sa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul dang /skyes bu dam pa la rtend pa dang /skyes bu dam par (pa)yin ba spong ba dang /chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid rtogs 
4b1(1): shing khong du chud par shes pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi sa dang /mkhas pa rnams kyis rjes su yi rang ba dang /byis ba rnams kyis spangs pa dang /nyan thos rnams kyis shes par dka' ba dang /rang sangs rgyas rnams kyis myi 
4b2(1): shes pa dang /mu stegs can gyi sa ma yin ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis yongs su bzung ba dang /stobs bcu dag gis rjes su rtogs pa dang /lha rnams kyis mchod par bya ba dang /tshangs pas phyag bya ba dang /brgya byin rnams kyis mngon tu 'gro 
4b3(1): bar bya ba dang /klu rnams kyis phyag bya ba dang /gnod sbyin rnams kyis rjes su yi rangabar bya ba dang /myi 'am ci rnams kyis bstod par bya ba dang /lto 'phye chen po rnams kyis bsngags par bya ba dang /byang chub semasadapa' rnams kyis bsgom bar bya ba dang /
4b4(1): mkhas pa rnams kyis kund chub par bya ba dang /bla na myed pa'i nor du gyurd pa dang /zang zing myed pa'i sbyin ba dang /nad pa rnams kyi sman du gyurd pa dang /ye shes kyi mdzod du gyurd pa dang /spobs pa myi zad pa dang /mdo sde rnams kyi tshul du gyurd pa dang /dpa' po 
4b5(1): dag gi g.yul du gyurd pa dang /khams gsum yongs su shes pa dang /pha rold tu 'gro ba dag la gzings lta bu dang /chu bo'i nang du zhugs pa dag la gru lta bu dang /grags pa 'dod pa dag gi grags pa dang /sangs rgyas rnams kyis bstod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs 
4b6(1): pa rnams kyis bsngags pa dang /stobs bcu rnams kyis bstod pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' dag gi yon tan dang /snying rje can dag gi btang snyoms dang /nyes pa zhi bar 'dod pa dag gi byams pa dang /theg pa chen po dag gi dbugs 'byind pa dang /seng ge'i sgra 
4b7(1): sgrogs pa rnams kyis nan tan dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyi lam dang /chos thams cad kyi phyag rgya dang /thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes yongs su sgrub pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bskyed mos tshal du gyurd pa dang /bdud kyi sde rnams skrag par byed pa 
4b8(1): dang /bde bar 'gro ba rnams kyi rig pa dang /don grub pa rnams kyi don dang /dgra'i nang du song ba dag gi skyabs dang phyir rgol ba rnams chos dang 'thun bas tshar gcod pa dang /myi 'jigs pa rnams kyi bden ba'i rnam pa dang /stobs rnams yang dag par tshol ba dang /
4b9(1): sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi snga ltas su gyurd pa dang /chos kyi sku'i rgyan dang /spyod pa rnams kyi rgyu 'thun ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi sras rnams kyi rgyand dang /thard pa 'dod pa rnams kyi dga' ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shas yongs su rdzogs pa dang 
4b10(1): nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sa ma yin ba dang /sems rnam par dag pa dang /lus yongs su dag pa dang /rnam par thard pa'i sgo rnams yongs su rdzogs pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kun nas nyon mongs pa myed pa dang /'dod chags 'byung 
4b11(1): ba myed pa dang /zhe sdang dang 'bral ba dang /gti mug gi sa ma yin ba dang /ye shes 'byung ba dang /rig pa skye ba dang /ma rig pa spong ba dang /rnam par grol ba snying por byed pa rnams kyi tshim ba dang /ting nge 'dzind snying por byed pa rnams kyis dga' ba dang /lta 'dod 
5a1(1): /   /rnams kyi myig tu gyurd pa dang /rnam par 'phrul bar 'dod pa rnams kyi mngon bar shes pa dang /mngon bar sgrub par 'dod pa rnams kyi 'phrul dang /thos pa don tu gnyer ba rnams kyi gzungs dang /dran ba myi brjed pa dang /
5a2(1): sangs rgyas rnams kyi byind kyi rlabs dang /'drend pa rnams kyi thabs mkhas pa dang /myi brtson ba rnams kyi phra ba dang /shes par dka' ba dang /ma grol ba dag gis myi shes pa dang /yi ge rnams ldog pa dang /sgras shes par dka' ba dang /
5a3(1): rtogs pa rnams kyi kun shes pa dang /des pa rnams kyis shes pa dang /'dod pa chung ba rnams kyis rab tu rtogs pa dang /brtson 'grus brtsams pa rnams kyis bzung ba dang /dran ba dang ldan ba rnams kyis bcas pa dang /sdug bsngal 
5a4(1): zad pa dang /chos thams cad skye ba myed pa dang /srid pa la 'gro ba dang /skye ba'i gnas thams cad gcig tu nges par bstand pa ste //gzho nu 'di ni chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin mnyam ba nyid rnam par spros pa'i ting nge 'dzind ces bya'o //
5a5(1): chos thams cad kyi rang bzhi mnyam ba nyid rnam par sprod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzind bstand pa 'di //bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad pa na /lha dang myi'i skye dgu sngon yongs su sbyang ba byas pa khrag khrig phrag brgyad cus myi skye ba'i chos la bzod 
5a6(1): pa thob bar gyurd to //khrag khrig phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug gis rjes su 'thun ba'i bzod pa thob par gyurd to //khrag khrigphrag dgu bcu rtsa gsum gyis sgra'i rjes su 'brang ba'i bzod pa thob par 'gyurd to //dge slong stong tshang ba lend pa myed 
5a7(1): pas zag pa las sems rnam par grol lo //lha dang myi'i skye dgu las srog chags stong phrag drug cus chos rnams la chos kyi myig rdul myed cing dri ma dang bral ba rnam par dag go //dge slong ma brgyad khri lend pa myed pas zag pa las sems rnam
5a8(1): par grol lo //dge bsnyend lnga brgyas phyir myi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu thob par gyurd to //dge bsnyen ma drug stong gis lan cig phyir lngo ba'i 'bras bu thob par 'gyurd to //stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di yang rnam pa drug du g.yos /
5a9(1): rab tu g.yos /kund tu rab tu g.yos /'guld rab tu 'guld /kund tu rab tu 'guld /ldeg rab tu ldeg /kund tu rab tu ldeg /'khrugs rab tu 'khrugs /kund tu rab tu 'khrugs /chem chem /kund tu rab tu chem chem /'ur 'ur rab tu 'ur 'ur 
5a10(1): shar phyogs dma' na nub phyogs mtho' /nub phyogs dma' na shar phyogs mtho' /byang phyogs dma' na lgo phyogs mtho' /lgo phyogs dma' na byang phyogs mtho' /mtha' dma' na dbus mtho' /dbus dma' na mtha' mtho' ste //tshad myed pa'i snang ba 'jig 
5a11(1): rtend du byung nas 'jig rtend gyi bar mun nag smag tu gyurd pa gang yin ba de dag kyang snang ba des gsal bar gyurd to //sems can gang dag der skyes pa de dag gcig gis gcig shes par gyurd to //'di skad du kye sems can gzhan dag kyang 'dir skyes para' 
5b1(1): gyurd to //zhes mdzerd te //mnar myed pa'i sems can dmyal ba chen po'i bar du yang de bzhin no ////gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o ////de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis /zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa la bka' stsald 
5b2(1): pa //gzho nu ngas dran ba sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa na /'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por gyurd te /ting nge 'dzind 'di 'dod cing myur du bla na myed ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon bar rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'dod pas /
5b3(1): bya rgod kyi phung po'i ri 'di nyid la bskald pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du mar de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas grangs myed dpag tu myed pa rnams la ngas rin po che thams cad dang //me tog dang /spos
5b4(1): dang /dri dang /'phreng ba dang /byug pa dang /phye ma dang /gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan dang /sil snyan dang /pheg brdab pa dang /rnam par rgyal ba'i ba dan dang /rin po che'i gtsug lag khang rnam pa sna tshogs rnam par bkra ba 
5b5(1): bye ba khrag khrig phrag brgya stong du ma'i bkod pa thams cad kyis bkur bstir byas /bla mar byas /ri mor byas /mchod par byas /rjed par byas /gsol bar byas te //gzho nu ngas de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad las chos thams 
5b6(1): cad kyi rang bzhin mnyam ba nyid rnam par spros pa'i ting nge 'dzind 'di rgyas par thos te //bzung ngo /dris so /bcangs so /pags so /rab tu btond to //nyon mongs pa myed pa'i sgom bas bsgoms so //mang du byas shing gzhan 
5b7(1): dag la yang rgya cher yang dag par rab tu bstand to //gzho nu de bzhin gshegs pa de dang thams cad kyi tha ma ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas stsa la'i dbang po'i rgyal po zhes bya ba zhig byung ste /gzho 
5b8(1): nu de bzhin gshegs pa stsa la'i dbang po'i rgyal bo de'i nyan thos dang byang chub sems dpa' khrag khrig brgya stong gi dge 'dun byung ste /de dag gi tshe'i tshad ni lo 'bum phrag bdun cu rtsa drug du gyurd to //gzho nu de bzhi gshegs pa dgra bcom pa 
5b9(1): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sa ngas rgyas stsa la'i dbang bo'i rgyal po de la ngas lo bye ba stong phrag bco brgyad du mchod pa dang rim 'gro byas te /lha'i tsan dan dang rin po'i che'i gtsug lag khang bye ba byas so //bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs
5b10(1): pa dgra bcom ba yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rtsa la'i dbang po'i rgyal po de'i tshe'i tshad ni lo bye ba stong phrag bdun cu rtsa drug tu gyurd to //bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dgapar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas
5b11(1): stsa la'i dbang po'i rgyal po de'i drung du nga rab tu byung nas lo bye ba stong phrag bcu bzhir chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin mnyam ba nyid rnam par spros pa'i ting nge 'dzind 'di mnyan cing bzung ste /dris so /bcangs so /pags so /rab tu btond to //nyon mongs 
6a1(1): /  /pa myed pa'i bsgom bas bsgoms so //mang du byas shing gzhan la yang rgya cher yang dag par rab tu bstand to //de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa la bka' stsald pa /gzho nu de lta bas na ting nge 'dzind 
6a2(1): 'di 'dod cing myur du bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon bar rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'dod na /khyod kyis kyang nga bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la mchod pa dang /rim gro dang /g.yog bya ba la brtson bar bya'o //gzho nu de ci'i phyir 
6a3(1): zhe na /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad la mchod pa dang /rim gro dang /g.yog byas pa'i rgyu 'thun bas bla na myed pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyang rnyed par dka' ba ma yin na /ting nge 'dzind 'di lta ci 
6a4(1): smos //gzho nu de lta bas na khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad la mchod pa dang /rim 'gro dang /g.yog bya ba ltag tu yid yongs su myi skyo bar bya'o //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe na /zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa la sngon byung ba'i le'u 'di 
6a5(1): nyid rgyas par tshigs su bcad pa'i dbyangs kyis rgya cher yang dag par rab tu bstand to ///dran ba stobs bcus bye ba drug cu dag //sngon che bya rgod phung po'i ri la bzhugs //nga sngon byang chub spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe //ting 'dzind zhi ba'i mchog 'di bshad 
6a6(1): bya ste //de dag gi yang tha ma ni //'jig rten mgon po 'od byed pa //stsa la'i dbang rgyal zhes bya ste //de la nga 'is yongs su dris //nga yang rgyal rigs nyid gyu rad te //rgyal po sa bdag gtso bo yin //nga'i bu yang lnga brgya ste //de dag las ni 
6a7(1): mchod pa dam pa dag //lo stong bye ba bco brgyad bar du byas //rnam par 'drend pa stsa la'i dbang po rgyal //rkang gis gtso bo rgyal ba ma smad pa //de'i dus na de'i tshe tshad ni //bye ba stong phrag bdun cu drug du gyurd //nyan thos mtshungs pa khrag khrig 
6a8(1): brgyad cu ste //gsum rig mngon shes drug ldan dbang po thuld //zag pa zad cing tha ma'i lus 'dzind pa //de tshe myi mchog dag gi dge 'dun byung //lhar bcas 'jig rten dag la phan ba'i phyir //de tshe ting 'dzind 'di ni bdag 'dod pa //rgyal ba 
6a9(1): rkang gnyis gtso bo de dag la //mchod pa rnam pa mang po ngas byas te //rgyal ba stsa la'i dbang rgyal drung du yang //nga ni btsun mo sras bcas rab tu byung //lo yang bye ba stong phrag bcu bzhir ni //ting nge 'dzind 'di ngas kyang yongs dris te //tshigs 
6a10(1): bcad khrag khrig stong phrag brgyad cu dang //gzhan yang dkrigs phrag bye ba brgya rnams ni //ting 'dzind 'di nyid kyis ni le'u gcig po //ngas ni bde bar gshegs pa de las blangs //ting 'dzind dam pa 'di ni 'dod pa'i phyir //mgo dang lag pa
6b1(1): chung pa de bzhin du //rin cen mang po de bzhin bza' bca' rnams //sngan cad ngas ni ma btang cung zad myed //sangs rgyas dag ni bye ba phrag stong dang //de las lhag par gang gis bye ma snyed //gang dag bya rgod phung po 'dir bzhugs nas //
6b2(1): ting 'dzind zhi mchog 'di stond ngas dran te //de kund mtshand kyang shag kya khyu mchog yin //kund kyi sras kyang sgra gcan zind ces bya //zham ring ba'i mying yang kun dga' bo //ser skya'i grong nas thams cad rab tu byung //phru thugs mchog
6b3(1): sha ri'i bu dang pang nas skyes //skyob pa kund kyang mtshan ni 'thun bar gyurd //de tshe 'jig rten khams kyang mying 'dra ste //thams cad kyang ni snyig+sa ma'i dus su byung //byang chub spyad pa 'di ni spyod pa'i tshe //myi dbang de kun nga yis bkur bsti 
6b4(1): byas //ting 'dzind 'di ni yongs su tshol ba'i tshe //rgyal la mchod pa ma byas ci yang myed //nan tan gyis ni ting 'dzind 'di rnyed de //nan tan dag kyang rnam pa mang du gsungs //yon tan kun la rab tu gnas pa dag //de la ting 'dzind 
6b5(1): 'di ni rnyed myi dka' //bro ba dag la ma zhend myi brkam zhing //khyim rnams la yang ma chags phrag dog myed //byams la gnas shing khro ba myed pa dag //de la ting 'dzanid 'di ci rnyed myi dka' //rnyed dang bkur bsti dag kyang myi 'dod cing //
6b6(1): 'tsho ba gtsang la ci yang myi 'chang ba //tshul khrims rnam par dag cing 'jigs myed pa //de la ting 'dzind 'di ni rnyed myi dka' /g.yel ba myed cing brtson 'grus brtsams pa dang //dgon par gnas shing sbyangs la gnas gyurd dang //bdag
6b7(1): myed bzod pa la yang rab gnas pa //de la ting 'dzin de ni rnyed myi dka' //rgod pa myed cing shind tu dul ba'i sems //spyod dang spyod lam dag la rab tu gnas //gtong la mos shing ser sna myed pa dag //de la tangi 'dzind 'di ni rnyed myi bka' //
6b8(1): 'ating 'dzind zhi ba 'di ni gang 'dzind pa //de la dpe' byad bzang dang mtshan rnams dang //'drend pa sangs rgyas chos ni bco brgyad dang //stobs dang myi 'jigs gang brjod rnyed par dka' //sangs rgyas spyan gyis gzigs pa'i sems can gang //
6b9(1): de dag dus gcig sangs rgyas nyid gyurd la //de rnams re re'i tshe 'di tshad kyang ni //bskald pa bye ba stong phrag bsam myi khyab //de dag re re la yang mgo bo ni //rgya mtsho' kund gyi bye ma de rnyed yod //mgo bo ci snyed yod pa thams cad la //de snyed 
6b10(1): lce yang mgo bo kun la yod //de dag kund kyis ting 'dzind 'di las gang //tshigs bcad 'dzind 'di'i phan yon brjod kyang ni //cung zad yongs su brjod par myi 'gyur na //gang gis bslabs shing gzung ba ci zhig smos //ting 'dzind 
6b11(1): blta dka' zhi ba gang 'dzind pa //sbyangs pa'i yon tan blangs zhing spyod pa dag //de la lha dang lha myin gnod sbyin smod //rgyal po dag kyang phyi bzhin 'brang bar byed //ting 'dzind zhi ba rnyed dka' gang 'dzind pa //rgyal ba dag gis yongasu 
7a1(1): //bzung bar 'gyur //lha dang klu rnams rtag tu rjes su 'brang //phas kyis rgol bas de'i gzi myi bzod //ting 'dzind zhi ba 'di ni gang 'dzind pa //de'i spobs pa dag ni mtha' yas 'gyur //mdo sde dag ni stong rnams rab 
7a2(1): stond kyang //de la nam yang rgyun myi 'chad myi 'gyur //gang rnams myi bzad chen po'i phyi dus na //ting nge 'dzind 'di thos nas 'dzind pa rnams //sangs rgyas 'drend pa spang ba dpag med phra//bde ldan 'jig rten khams kyang mthong bar 
7a3(1): 'gyur //phan yon 'di dag rab tu bstand mdzad nas //yongs su mya ngan nga 'das phyi ma'i tshe //ting 'dzind rnam par dag pa 'di zung zhes //rang byung stond pa bdag nyid gsol ba 'debs //phyogs bcu mya ngan gang 'das sangs rgyas dang 
7a4(1): gang rnams ma byond da ltar byung ba yi //rgyal ba thams cad ting 'dzind 'di bslabs nas //byang chub rdul bral 'dus ma byas pa rtogs ////rang byung stond pa bdag nid gsol ba 'debs ///phyogs bcu mya ngan gangs 'das sangs rgyas dang /
7a5(1): rgyal po'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o //////de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis //zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa la bka' stsald pa //gzho nu de lta bas na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos de bzhin 
7a6(1): gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi yon tan brjod pa yang dag par bstand pa dang /don dang 'bru yongs su myi zad par bya ba dang //bdag gi tshig thams cad sangs rgyas kyis yongs su bzung 
7a7(1): zhing 'byung bar 'dod na //gzho nu byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po des sems can thams cad kyi dond tu ting nge 'dzind 'di gzung bar bya //kun chub par bya /bcang bar bya /klag par bya /rab tu gdon par bya /lung mnod par bya /
7a8(1): kha ton du bya /nyon mongs pa myed pa'i bsgom bas bsgom bar bya /mang du bya /gzhan dag la yang rgya cher rab tu bstand par bya'o //gzho nu de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi yon tan yang dag pa gang yin zhe na /gzho nu 'di la byang chub 
7a9(1): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dgon pa na 'dug kyang rung /shing drung na 'dug kyang rung /bla gab myed pa na 'dug kyang rung /khyim stong pa na 'dug kyang rung /'di la yang dag par slob ste //'di ltar bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 
7a10(1): dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig pa dang zhabs su ldan ba /bde bar gshegs pa /'jig rten mkhyend pa /skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba /bla na myed pa /lha dang myi rnams kyi stond pa /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de 
7a11(1): bzhin gshegs pa de bsod nams dag gi rgyu 'thun ba /dge' ba'i rtsa ba rnams chud myi za ba /bzod pas brgyand pa /bsod nams kyi gter rnams kyi gzhi /dpe' byad bzang po rnams kyis spras pa /mtshan gyi me tog rgyas pa spyod yul rand bar 
7b1(1): 'thun ba /mthong na myi 'thun pa myed pa /dad pas mos pa rnams mngon bar dga' ba /shes rab zild kyis myi nond pa /stobs rnams la rdzi ba myed pa /sems can thams cad kyi stond pa /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yab /
7b2(1): 'phags pa'i gang zag rnams kyi rgyal po /las dang po pa rnams kyi ded dpon ye shes dpag tu myed pa /spobs pa bsam gyis myi khyab pa /gsung rnam par dag pa /dbyangs snyand pa /sgyu byad lta bas chog myi shes pa /sku mtshungs pa myed 
7b3(1): pa /'dod pa dag gis ma gos pa /gzugs dag gis nye bar ma gos pa /gzugs myed pa dag dang ma 'dres pa /phung po dag las rab tu rnam par grol ba /khams rnams dang myi ldan ba /skye mched rnams bsdams pa /mdud pa rnams shind tu bcad
7b4(1): pa /yongs su gdung ba dag las rnam par grol ba /spyod pa las yongs su grol ba /chu bo las rgald pa /ye shes yongs su rdzogs pa /'das pa dang ma byond pa dang da ltar gyi /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi ye shes la gnas pa /
7b5(1): mya ngan las 'das pa la myi gnas pa /yang dag pa nyid kyi mtha' la gnas pa /sems can thams cad la gzigs pa'i sa la bzhugs pa ste /gzho nu 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi yon tan yang dag pa ste /byang chub sems dpa' 
7b6(1): sems dpa' chen po sangs rgyas kyi yon tan dang bsngags pa 'di dag dang ldan zhing /ting nge 'dzind 'di la brten nas /spobs pa rgyun myi chad pas de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi sangs rgyas 
7b7(1): kyi yon tan dang bsngags par yang dag par bstand na /don dang 'bru yongs su zad par myi 'gyur te /de'i tshig thams cad kyang sangs rgyas kyis yongs su bzung bar 'byung ngo //de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis don de nyid gsal bar bya ba'i phyir /zla 'od 
7b8(1): gzho nur gyurd pa la rgyas par tshigs su bcad pa'i dbyangs kyis rgya cher yang dag par rab tu bshad do //bskald pa stong phrag mang por rab bshad kyang //rgyal ba'i bsngags pa thams cad brjod myi lang //ting 'dzind zhi mchog 'di ni tshol ba'i phyir //rgyal 
7b9(1): ba de lta'i yon tan yang dag bsgrubs //ngas ni ting 'dzind zhi mchog 'di tshol bas //sngon tshe zhum ba myed pa'i yid kyis su //bu mo gzugs bzang shind tu blta na sdug /legs par lus brgyan yid du 'ong ba byin //ngas ni ting 'dzind zho mchog 'di tshol
7b10(1): bas //sngon tshe zhum ba med pa'i yid kyis su //de bzhin bran po bran mo nor dang 'bru //de bzhin nor bu mu tig gser dngul byin //ngas ni ting 'jind zhi mchog 'di tshol bas//nor bu rin cen sna tshogs mu tig 'phreng //gos dang gdu bu gser skud mdzes pa 
7b11(1): dag //sngon tshe 'drend pa rnams la nges btang byas //bskald pa bye ba mtha' yas dpag med par //dri mchog dri ldan dbyangad kyi me tog dag //rab tu bla na myed pa'i sems bskyed nas //rgyal ba'i mchod rten dag la nga yis phul //'khord kyi nang du 
8a1(1): //ri mo bskyed nas kyang //de bzhin chos kyi sbyin ba ngas byin te //chos kyi sbyin ba byin bas nga grags snyam //nga la nam yang de 'dra'i sems ma skyes //sdug pa'i mchog byin bdag nyid spyad par ni //de snyam chags pa nga la yong 
8a2(1): ma byung //sbyin ba'i chos ni mang du ngas byin te //rtag tu sangs rgyas ye shes ngas thos snyam //sbyangs pa'i yon tan dag kyang ngas sngon bsgrubs //gnas mchog sgra snyan rtag tu gnas byas te //thams cad dus su snying rje mang bar gyurd //
8a3(1): rtag tu sangs rgyas ye shes ras 'thob snyam //byams shing 'jam la bged pa'i ngang tshul can //thos pa 'dzind cing bzhin 'dzum tshig kyang 'jam //shind tu tshig snyand mang po kund kyang dga' //nga la skye bo kun lta ngoms myi 'gyur //ser sna 
8a4(1): can du skad cig ma gyurd te //srid pa mang por nam yang ser sna myed //rtag tu bsod snyoms dag la nga dga' ste //mgron tu bos pa thams cad ma lus spangs //'di las rtsa bzhi tshigs bcad gcig bzung na //mang du thos shing thos pa 'dzind par 
8a5(1): 'gyur //des ni nga la bla na myed pa yi //dad pa mchog bskyed rtag tu bkur bsti byas //ngas ni ting 'dzind zhi mchog 'di tshol phyir //rnam par 'drend la mchod pa mang po byas //sbyin ba rnam pa mang po mtha' yas byin //de bzhin tshul khrims dag kyang 
8a6(1): yun ring bsrungs //'jig rten khams yangs sna tshogs mtha' yas pa //nor bu rin cen bkang ste sbyin byin bas //ting 'dzind 'di las tshigs bcad gcig bzu4 na //de bas bsod nams 'di ni rgya cher 'phags //bskald pa mtha' yas dpag 
8a7(1): myed mang por yang //me tog mang po ci snyed yod pa dang //de bzhin spos rnams yid 'ong rgya che bas //bsod nams 'dod phyir rgyal ba mchod pa dang //bskald pa mtha' yas1 dpag myed mang por ni //sil snyan mang po ci snyed yod pa da //de bzhin mnar 
8a8(1): mtshald zhal zas na bza' yis //bsod nams 'dod phir rgyal ba mchod pa bas //rang byung rgyal ba chos rjer bdag gyurd ces //myi gag byang chub du ni sems bskyed nas //mnyam bar bzhag pas tshigs bcad gcig bzung na //de bas bsod nas 
8a9(1): 'di ni rgya cher 'phags /gang ga'i bye ma mang po ci snyed pa //bskald pa de snyed du 'ang phan yon rnams //brjod kyang yongs su zad par myi nus te //ting 'dzind 'di bzung bsod nams shind tu che //de phyir sangs rgyas phan yon dag //shind tu bzang 
8a10(1): po 'di thos nas //sangs rgyas bsngags pa'i ting 'dzind 'di //myur du lung yang mnod par gyis //sangs rgyas bye ba bdun cu gsum //sngon gyi skye bar bkur bsti byas //sangs rgyas de dag thams cad kyis //mdo sde 'di ni rab tu bshad //mdo sde 
8a11(1): 'di'i mying yang ni //snying rje chen por 'jug ces bya //'di la mang du thos bslabs na //sangs rgyas chos ni rnyed myi dka' //'jig rten 'drend pa mya ngan 'das //phyi ma'i dus la dge slong ni //sdom pa myed pa mang po dag //mang du thos pa myi 'dod ra 
8b1(1): 'byung //tshul khrims sngags pa rjod byed la //tshul khrims don tu gnyer ba myed //ting 'dzind bsngags pa brjod byed la //ting 'dzind don tu gnyer ba myed //shes rab bsngags pa brjod byed la//shes rab dond tu gnyer ba myed //
8b2(1): rnam grol bsngags pa rjed byed la //rnam grol dond tu gnyer ba myed //ci ltar skyes bu la la dag //tsan dan nags ni 'di 'dra ste //spos kyi rnam pa yid 'ong zhes //tsan dan dag gi yon tan rjod //de nas myi gzhan la la yis //de ltar bsngags
8b3(1): pa brjod pa yi //tsan dan cung zad srel tam zhes //skyes bu la las de dris na //skyes bu de la slar smras pa //gang gis 'tsho bar byed pa yi //spos kyi sngags pa nga brjod pa //spos de nga la myed ces smra //de bzhin rnal 'byord myi brtson ba //
8b4(1): tshul khrims bsngags pas 'tsho ba dag //phyi ma'i dus la 'byung gyurd te //de dag la ni tshul khrims myed //de bzhin rnal 'byord mi brtson ba //ting 'dzind bsngags pas 'tsho ba dag//phyi ma'i dus la 'byung 'gyur te //de dag la ni ting 'dzind 
8b5(1): myed //de bzhin rnal 'byor da myi brtson ba //shes rab bsngags pas 'tsho ba dag  //phyi ma'i dus la 'byung
8b6(1): 'byung 'gyur te //de dag ni rnam grol myed //ci ltar skyes bu dbul po la la zhig //skye bo mang pos yongs su brnyas gyurd la //de yis phyis gdod nor gyi gter rnyed nas //nor yod rig pas skye bo ri mo byed //de bzhin ting 'dzind 'di ni 
8b7(1): mthong ba na //byang chub sems dpa' de 'ang lha myi dang //grul bum srin po mang pos myi bkur te //ci ltar skyes bu dbul pa nor myed bzhin //mkhas pas gzhal du myed pa'i chos kyi gter //sa 'di nam zhig rab tu thob gyurd na //lha dang myi dag 
8b8(1): de la re ba skyed //des kyang skye dgu bla myed nor rnams byin //de phyir rgyal ba dag gis brjod pa yi //phan yon dam pa bzang po 'di thos nas //shes kyi khe dang bde rnyed kun spangs te //ting 'dzind zhi mchog 'di ni lung mnod kyis //sangs 
8b9(1): rgyas su dag phyogs su mya ngan 'das //gang dag da ltar byung dang ma 'ongs pa //de dag thams cad ting 'dzind 'di bslabs nas //byang chub bsam pa mtshungs myed rnam sangs rgyas //zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa sems dga' nas //sangs rgyas 
8b10(1): spyan sngar 'dug ste tshig smras pa //skyes bu dam pa mya ngan yongs 'das nas //ma rungs dus na bdag gis mdo 'di gzung //bdag gis lus dang srog kyang yongs btang ste //'jig rten skyid pa ci mchis kun btang la //bdag gis 'jigs 
8b11(1): pa chen po'i dus der yang //ting 'dzind zhi mchog ni gzung bar gyis //sems can mang la snying rje cher bskyed de //sems can sdug bsngal mgon myed mthong nas kyang //de dag la ni byams pa nyer sbyar bzhin //ting 'dzind zhi mchog di ni'i 
9a1(1): //gzung bar bgyi //de'i tshe na ting 'dzind 'dzind pa dag //lnga brgya las ni myi nyung der 'byung ste //mdo sde mchog 'di rgya cher gzung ba la //de dag sngon tu 'drend pa gzho nu yin ////sangs rgyas kyi 
9a2(1): yon tan yang dag par bsngags pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o ////'phags pa ting nge 'dzind kyi rgyal po zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo ////bam po gnyis pa'o ////de nas 
9a3(1): zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa stan las langs te /bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bzard nas /pus mo g.yas pa'i lha ba la btsugs te /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyard pa btud nas /bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to //gal te 
9a4(1): zhus nas zhu ba lung bstand pa'i slad du /bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la skabs phye na /bdag bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la phyogs 'ga' zhig zhu'o //de skad ces gsold pa dang /
9a5(1): bcom ldan 'das kyis zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pal 'di skad ces bka' stsald to //gzho nu khyod la de bzhin gshegs pas rtag tu skabs phyes /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcam ba yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas la khyod ci dang ci 'dod pa dris shig /
9a6(1): de skad ces bka' stsald te /bcom ldan 'das kyis skabs phye ba dang /bcom ldan 'das la zla 'od gzho nur gyuradapa la 'di skad ces gsoladato //bcom ldan 'das ting nge 'dzind ting nge 'dzind ces bgyi ba de /chos gang gi tshig bla dgas 
9a7(1): lags /de skad ces gsold pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis zla 'dod gzho nur gyurd pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to //gzho nu ting nge 'dzind ting nge 'dzand ces bya ba ni 'di lta ste //sems nges par rtog pa dang /skye ba myed pa dang /
9a8(1): mtshams sbyor ba myed pa dang /mtshams sbyor ba shes pa dang /khur bor ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes dang /sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog du gyurd pa dang /'dod chags gso ba dang /zhe sdang nye bar zhi ba dang /gti mug bsal ba dang /rigs 
9a9(1): par rnal 'byord du byed pa dang /myi rigs pa rnam par spong ba dang /dge' ba'i chos la 'dund pa dang /'khor ba las thard par 'dod pa dang /lhag pa'i bsam ba sgrub pa dang /myi nyal ba la rtend pa dang /spong ba myi gtong ba dang /chos dkar po 
9a10(1): rnams yongs su srung ba dang /skye bo rnams la yid myi rtond pa dang /las rnams la mngon par 'du myi byed pa dang /nang gi skye mched rnams yid la myi byed pa dang /phyi ma'i skye mched rnams la kund tu myi spyod pa dang /bdag la myi stod pa dang /
9a11(1): gzhan la myi smod pa dang /khyim la chags pa myed pa dang /so so'i skye bo la yid myi rtond pa dang tshul khrims kyi rgyu 'thun pa ba dang /bsnyend par dka' ba dang /gzi chen po dang /bdag shes pa dang /myi rgod pa dang /spyod lam phun sum tshogs 
9b1(1): par gnas pa dang /gnod sems myed pa dang /myi rtsub pa dang /gzhan la myi gnod pa dang /bshes gnyen rnams la rjes su srung ba dang /gsang ba'i sngags kun srung ba dang /tshul khrims dang ldan ba rnam par myi 'tshe zhing gnod pa myed pa dang /
9b2(1): 'jam por smra ba dang /khams gsum thams cad la myi gnas pa dang /chos thams cad la stong pa nyid kyi rjes su 'thun ba'i bzod pa dang /thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes la shind tu 'dund pa ni /gzho nu ting nge 'dzind ces bya ste /gzho nu gang 
9b3(1): de lta bu'i chos de dag la sgrub par byed gyi log par sgrub par myi byed pa 'di ni ting nge 'dzind ces bya'o //de nas bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin mnyam ba nyid rnam par spros pa'i ting nge 'dzind 'di nyid stond cing //de'i tshe zla 'od 
9b4(1): gzho nur gyurd pa la rgyas par tshigs su bcad pa'i dbyangs kyis rgya cher yang dag par rab tu bstand to //ngas ni bdud rtsi'i sgo yang rab tu phye //chos kyi rang bzhin ci 'dra de bzhin bshad //skye ba dag kyang ci 'dra ngas bstand te //mya ngan 'das 
9b5(1): pa phan yon bcas pa bshad //sdig pa'i grogs po rtag tu rnam par spangs //dge' ba'i bshes gnyen la yang bsnyend par bya //'du ba spangs te nags khrod gnas bca' zhing //byams pa'i sems ni rtag tu bsgom bar bya //tshul khrims dag pa 
9b6(1): rtag tu bsrung bya ste //sbyangs la dga' ba 'ang rtag tu thob par bya //gtong ba dang ni 7shes rab bsnyen byas na //ting 'dzind 'di ni rnyed par dka' myi 'gyur /7nyan thos mang po'i ni sa myin ba //zhi ba'i sa 'di dag kyang thos nas ni //bde bar gshegs 
9b7(1): pa'i chos la mngon sum gyurd //sangs rgyas yon tan bsam yas thob par 'gyur //skyes bu blo ldan snod gyurd mthong nas kyang //de dag byang chub sems la yang dag tshud //bla myed ye shes la ni rab bkod na //ting 'dzind rgyal po 'di ni rnyed myi dka' //
9b8(1): yongs su tshol dang kund tu longs spyod dang //kha zas rgyu 'thun dag ni gang gi phyir //phrag dog skyes pa de la rab tu rtogs //ting 'dzind 'di nirnyed par yong myi dka' //ting 'dzind rgyal por stong pa 'di sgom ba //tshul khrims dag pa'i mgo la de 'dug ste //
9b9(1): chos rnams rang bzhin rtag tu mnyam bzhag na //byis ba myi rigs brtson bas myi shes so //su dag ting 'dzind zhi ba 'di 'dod pa //de la nam yang 'jigs pa'i blo myi 'byung //rab tu zhi ba'i sa 'di bstend nas ni //myi mchog rnams kyang rtag tu mthong 
9b10(1): bar 'gyur //de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku byad gang dran ba //de ni yid zhi dbang po zhi bar 'gyur //sems la 'khrul myed rtag tu mnyam bar bzhag /thos dang shes pa dag ni rgya mtsho' bzhin //ting 'dzind 'di la rab tu gnas nas ni //byang chub sems 
9b11(1): 'dapa' 'chag sar su 'chag pa //des ni sangs rgyas bye ba phrag stong dang //de bas lhag par gang ga'i bye snyed mthong //su dag sangs rgyas chos la tshad 'dzind pa //myi' de'i sems ni shind tu myos par 'gyur //tshad las 'das pa la ni tshad gzung myed /
10a1(1): //'drend pa yon tan thams cad bsam myi khyab //phyogs bcu dag na sems can su yang ni //'jig rten mgon dang mnyam myed su zhig lhag //thams cad mkhyend pa'i yon tan kun ldan ba //sangs rgyas ye shes 'dod
10a2(1): cing thob par gyis //sku lus dag ni gser gyi mdog 'dra bas //'jig rten mgon po kun nas rab tu mdzes //dmyigs pa 'di la gang gi sems 'jug pa //byang chub sems dpa' de ni mnyam bzhag yin //mkhas pas 'dus byas 
10a3(1): 'dus ma byas rig ste //mtshan ma'i 'du shes de ni rnam bshig nas //mtshan ma myed pa la ni de gnas na //chos rnams thams cad stong par rab tu shes //gang dag chos kyi sku la gnas gyurd pa //de ni dngos po thams cad dngos myed shes //
10a4(1): de yis dngos po'i 'du shes rnam bshig nas //rgyal ba'i dbang po gzugs kyi dngos myi lta //khyed la bsgo zhing khong du chud par bya //ci ltar myi dag mang du rtog byed pa //de la gnas pa'i rtog pa de dag gis //de bzhin du ni de la sems 
10a5(1): gzhol 'gyur //de bzhin rgyal ba'i yi ni sku byad dang //ye shes dpag tu myed par thub dbang dran //rtag par rjes su dran ba sgom byas na //de la sems gzhol rab tu 'bab par 'gyur //de ni 'chag 'dug 'greng zhing gnas pa'i tshe //
10a6(1): skyes bu dam pa'i ye shes rab tu 'dod //'jig rten rgyal ba bla myed bdag 'gyur bara //'dod pas byang chub du yang smon lam 'debs //des ni sangs rgyas shes shing sangs rgyas mthong //sangs rgyas nyid nyid la yang so sor rtog //ting 
10a7(1): 'dzind 'di la rab tu gnas byas nas //mthu' cen sangs rgyas la ni phyag kyang 'tshal //lus dang ngag dang yid rab dang ba yis //rtag tu sangs rgyas dag gi bsngags pa rjod //de ltar des ni sems kyi rgyud bsgoms pas //nyin mtshan 
10a8(1): du yang 'jig rten mgon po mthong //nam zhig de ni na zhing myi bde nas //shi la thug pa'i sdug bsngal tshor byung na //sangs rgyas la ni dran ba myi nyams te //sdug bsngal tshor bas 'grogs par yong myi 'gyur //de ltar de dag ye shes rnam 
10a9(1): brtags pas //'ongs dang ma 'ongs chos rnams stong par shes //de 'dra'i chos kyi tshul la de gnas pas //spyod pa yongs su spyod pas sems myi skyo //de phyir phan yon 'di dag thos nas ni //mtshungs myed byang chub la yang mos pa skyed //bder 
10a10(1): gshegs dam pa mthong ba rnyed dka' yis //phyi ma'i dus na 'gyod pa skyes sa re //ngas ni rab tu bzang ba'i chos bshad kyang //khyed kyis thos nas yang dag ma spyad na //nad pa sman gyi rkyald pa thogs pa dag //bdag gi nad ni gso bar ma nus bzhin //
10a11(1): de phyir mkhas pa shind tu rtogs pa dag //rtag tu ting 'dzind 'di ni 'dod pa yis //tshul khrims thos pa gtong ba bsnyen byas na //ting 'dzind 'di ni rnyed par yong myi dka' ////ting nge 'dzind kyi le'u ste bzhi pa'o //
10b1(1): de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis //zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa la bka' stsald pa //gzho nu de lta bas na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos ting nge 'dzin 'di 'dod cing myur du bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon 
10b2(1): bar rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'dod pas //mgo dang gos la mye 'bar ba bzhin du //sems kyis pha dang ma dang bu dang bu mo dang nye du dang //snag gi gnyen mtshams dang /gnyen dang rtsa lag dang chung ma las stsogs pa mye bzhin du spangs la /
10b3(1): rgyal srid kyi bde ba thams cad mchil ma'i thald pa bzhin du bor te /dgon pa la mngon tu phyogs shing khyim gyi gnas nas mngon bar 'byung bar bya'o //de ci'i phyir zhe na /gzho nu dgon pa la mngon tu phyogasashing khyim gyi gnas nas mdon bar byung ba'i byang chub 
10b4(1): sems dpa' sems dpa' che na pos bla na myed pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyang rnyed par dka' ba ma yin na //ting nge 'dzind 'di lta ci smos te /gzho nu de lta bas na /bu dang mdza' bo dang /nye du dang snag gi gnyen mtshams dang/gnyen dang /rtsa lag 
10b5(1): dang chung ma las stsogs ba mye bzhin du spangs la/rgyal srid kyi bde ba thams chad mchil ma'i thald pa bzhi du bord te /dgond pa la mngon tu phyogs shid /khyim gyi gnas nas mngonabar 'byung bar bya'o zhes //gzho nu khyod kyis de ltar rtag tu bslab 
10b6(1): par bya'o /gzho nu de lta bas rnam grangs 'dis kyang khyod kyis 'di ltar rig par bya ste/gzho nu sngon byung ba 'das pa'i dus na /bskald pa grangs myed pa bas kyang ches grangs myed pa /rgya che ba dpag tu myed pa /bsam gyis myi khyab pa /
10b7(1): gzhal du myed par gyurd pa de'i tshe de'i dus na /bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dbyangs byin zhes bya ba rig pa dang zhabs su ldan ba /bde bar gshegs pa /'jig rtend mkhyend pa /
10b8(1): skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba /bla na myed pa /lha dang myi rnaMs kyi stond pa /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten du byung ngo //gzho nu de'i tshe de'i dus na //bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa dgra bcom ba yangadag par rdzogs pa'i sangs

14b2(1): dad myed pa //de dag byang chub dam pa rnyed myi nus //tshul nyams pa'i so so'i skye bo dang //gang dag chos la dad pa myed pa dang //tshangs pa mtshungs spyod dag la ri mo myed //de dag byang chub 'di ni rtogs myi nus //tshul ma nyams shing khrel 
14b3(1): yod ngo tsha shes //chos dang sangs rgyas dag la gang dga' zhing //tshangs pa mtshungs spyo dag la rab tu gus //de dag byang chub dmapa mchog thob 'gyur //gang dag spyod yul dran pa nye bar bzhag //bde zhing dga' ba'i lam du zhugs pa dang //

14b6(1): srid du ni tshe'i tshad gyurd nas //ngas ni sangs rgyas kyi yang ye shes kyis //su gcig bsngags pa brjod kyang mthard myi phyind //de phyir zild kyis myi non rgyal ba yis //bshad pa'i phan yon 'di dag thos nas ni //ting 'dzind 'di ni myur du

15a3(1): ldan 'das kyis /zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa la bzod pa gsum la 'jugapa'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di'i tshigs su bcad pa'i dbyangs kyis yang dag par rab tu bstanad to ///su dag dang yang rtsod par myi byed cing //don myed ldan ba'i tshig rnams myi smra ste //

15a5(1): par ro //mdo sde'i tshul kund la yang de mkhas shing //legs par smras la mkhas pa shind tu mos //bde gshegs mkhyend la mtha' yas ye shes can //'di dag bzod pa pa dang po'i khyad par ro //legs par bshad pa'i chos ni gang thos kyang //
15a6(1): sangs rgyas dag gis bshad par the tsom myed //rgyal ba kun gyi chos la de mos te //'di dag bzodapa dang po'i khyad par ro //stong pa bde bar gshegs pas bsha Ipa ltar //nges don mdo sde dag gi bye brag shes //gang las sems can gang 
15a7(1): zag skyes bu bstand //chos de thams cad drang ba'i don tu shes //'jig rten 'di na mu stegs mang yod pa //de dag la yang de ni yid myi khro //de dag la ni snying rje nye bar 'jog //'di dag bzod pa dang po'i khyad bar ro //de la gzugs rnams yongs su 
15a8(1): snang bar 'gyur //snang ba de la nam yang the tsom myed //bden ba'i rjes su 'brang ba'i tshig smras te //'di dag bzod padang po'i khyad par ro /rlung dang chu dang mye dang sa rnams kyi //khams bzhi po ni rnam pa gzhan 'gyur yang //sangs rgyas byang chub las ni 
15a9(1): de myi ldog //'di dag bzod dang po'i khyad par ro //'jig rten bzo' gnas mang po ci yod pa //thams cad byang chub sems dpa' des bslabs te //bdag las lhag pa su yang myi mthong ba //'di dag bzod pas dang po'i khyad par ro //zhi gnasa' 
15a10(1): stobs kyis bskyod pa myed gyurd cing //lhag mthong gis ni ri bo lta bur 'gyur //sems can kund kyis 'khrugs par myi nus te //de ni bzod pa gnyis su bshad pa yin //mnyam bar bzhag bzhin du ni ldang zhing smra //mnyam bar bzhag pas 'dug 
15a11(1): cing 'chag par byed //mkhas pa ting nge 'dzind kyi pha rold phyind //'di dag bzod pa gnyis pa'i khyad bar ro //mnyam bar bzhag pa mngon shes lnga 'thob ste //chos rnams stond cing zhing ni brgyar yang 'gro //de ni rdzu 'phrul stobs las myi nyaMs 
15b1(1): te //'di dag bzod pa gnyis pa'i khyad par ro //de ni de 'dra'i ting 'dzind zhi ba bstend //mnyam bar bzhag pa de'i sems kyi chod //'dzind nus pa'i sems can gang yang myed //'di dag bzod pa gnyis pa'i khyad par ro //'jig rten
15b2(1): 'di na gang yod sems can kun //de dag sangs rgyas mkhyend pas chos stond kyang //bshad pa de dag thams cad 'dzind nus te //'di dag bzod pa gnyis pa'i khyad par ro //shar dang lho dang nub dang byang phyogs dang //steng dang 'og dang

21b11(1): kyis shind tu brgyand pa /lha'i gser gyi kha dog lta bu /zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa'i spyi bor bzhag go //bcom ldan 'das kyis zla 'od gzho nur gyurd ba'i spyi bor phyag g.yas pa bzhag ma thag tu /skad cig de nyid la /zla 'od gzho nur gyur 
22a1(1): /pa la dpa' bar 'gro ba dang /nam mkha' mdzod dang /rdo rje lta bu dang /yid kyi mgyogs pa dang /gzugs thams cad stond pa dang /sgrib pa myed pa'i mtha' dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad stond pa dang/stong pa nyid

22a4(1): pa lcag kyu'i mtshan mnga' zhing //dpal be'u 'khor lo rgyal mtshan ba dan nya rim mnga' //bskald pa bye mang rin cen gtong la zhugs pa'i phyag //bcom ldan 'das kyis zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa'i spyi bor bzhag //de nas lha yul pa rgya che zhing dbe' myed pa /
22a5(1): sangs rgyas dang byang chub sesam dpa' thams cad kyi spyodayul du rig pa'i dga' ba dang bde ba dang ldan bar gyurd nas //stan las langs te bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bzard nas //pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de

22b5(1): dga' bar byas te /lha'i me tog ud pa la dang /pad ma dang /ku mu ta dang /pad ma dkar po dang /dri mchog dang /a ti mug gta' dang /mal li ka dang /tsam pa ka dang /skya snar dang /kun da dang /ti la ka dang /ke sa ra dang /mya ngan 'tshang

22b9(1): pa rnams bkod /lam gyi ngos gnyis su shing ta la mthon po lha'i rin po che sna tshogs dang /'dzam bu'i chu bo'i gser las byas pa'i dril bu g.yer ka'i dra ba bres shing sgra 'khrol bas brgyand pa rnams dang /rin po che sna tshogs kyi mkha'

23a1(1): //gos bzang khrag khrig rnam mar sna tshogs dang //me tog 'phreng ba bzang zhing mdzes pa yis //lam mchog yangs pa de ni rab tu brgyand //grong mchog gtsang ma 'dir ni thugs rje can //sangs rgyas thub pa'i zla 
23a2(1): ba gshegs so zhes //zla 'od gzho nus dad pa rab bskyed nas //rgya che yangs pa'i lam ni shind tu brgyand //lha rdzas kyi ni bye ma gtsang ma bdald //rab tu yid 'ong me tog mchod rnams bkram //rin cen rta babs dag kyang leg+sa

25a1(1): //brgya stong du ma ni mngon bar bsgreng /gdugs dang kha dog rags pa du ma rnam pa sna tshogs kyi ngang pa dang /khrung khrung dang /ngur pa dang /gzhad dang /tsa ko ra dang /rma bya dang /tsa sha dang /ri skegs dang /khu byug dang /ka la bing ka
