25a4(1): bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du ma dag gi lag pa gnyis na gtun 'dzind pa bzhin du thogs ste /spos sbrul gyi snying po 'go shir sha dang /mri na la dang /ha ri dang /ma la ya dang /tsan dan dmar po dang /a ga ru las stsogs pas bkang ba'i du ba ni 'thul /
25a5(1): phyogs so so nas lhags pa'i rgyal po dbang che ba /dbang che bar grags pa rnams rta dang /bal glang rgyand kyis shind tu brgyand cing ma zhon bar khrid pa'i tshogs du ma dang /shing rta dang dpung bu chung gis yongs su bskor cing 'phreng ba dang /lda ldi mang po 
25a6(1): dang /rin po che'i 'phreng ba lag na thogs shing sngon logs nas rkang gis 'gro'o //rta dang glang po che legs par brgyand pa ma zhon bar khrid pa dag kyang rang gi stabs kyis dngom bar 'gro zhing skad yid du 'ong la snyand pa 'byind to //tshangs
25a7(1): pa yang bcom ldan 'das kyi g.yas phyogs nas mchod pa dang /rim 'gro bsnyend bkur byed cing 'gro'o //g.yon phyogs nas ni lha'i dbang po brgya byin lha'i bu dbang che ba dbang che bar grags pa rgya che zhing rlabs che ba bgrang ba las 'das pa' 
25a8(1): rnams lha'i cod pan bzang po dang /gdu bu rgyan can dang /rna ca dang /gdu bu dang /me tog rna rgyan dang /rin po che'i tsong tsong dang /me tog gi 'phreng bas lus brgyand cing gzugs mngon bar snang bas /lha'i me tog man da ra dang /mdog mdzes 
25a9(1): dang /ko bi da ra dang /s+tha la ka dang /ku ra ba ka dang /tsam pa ka dang /dong ka'i me tog dang /mya ngan 'tshang dang /skya snar dang /a d+hi mug ta dang /ud pal dang /ba ku la dang /mal li ka dang /kun da dang /ke sa ra dang 
25a10(1): pad ma dang /ku mu da dang /pad ma dkar po dang /dri mchog dang /rin po che pad ma'i 'phreng ba dang /rin po che'i a ga ru dang /shing rin po che'i 'bras bu can dag lag na thogs shing /sil snyan sna tshogs brgya stong ni shind tu bkrold /
25a11(1): gos bye ba ni g.yob cing a la la zhes ga zha'i sgra dag ni 'byind /me tog gi char chen po ni rab tu 'bebs shing //bcom ldan 'das mchod pa bya ba'i phyir /nam mkha'i ngos thams cad bar mtshams myed par gang bar 'khod do //
25b1(1): me tog dang spos dang 'phreng ba dang /byug pa dang /phye ma dang /rin po che'i char rab tu 'bebs shing /bcom ldan 'das chu dron yod pa'i sgo nas /rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen por gdugs tshod la gshegs so //de'i chos nyid 'di yin 
25b2(1): te /de la 'di skad ces bya'o //sangs rgyas de bzhin gshegs pas dus mkhyend nas //dpa' bo yon tan kund kyis rab brgyand pa //zhing rnams bsam gyis myi khyab snang mdzad cing //bcom ldan myi yi seng ge gshegs 
25b3(1): par gyurd //spyan ras gzigs dang mthu' cen don yod dang //spos kyi bal glang rin cen lag bzangs dang //rin cen tog dang 'byung dka' dpa' bo dang //de dang gzhan dag rgyal ba'i zham 'bring byed //g.yas phyogs na ni myi 
25b4(1): 'pham 'dug gyurd te //de mying byams pa yon tan mtha' yas phyug //bar du ma chod sangs rgyas 'gyur ba ste //bsod nams ye shes tshogs kyang rab tu rdzogs //byams dang btang snyoms dga' bas lus bsgoms shing //chos kyi bdag nyid 
25b5(1): chen po snying rjes bskyed //yon tan mtha' yas thub dbang yon tan sems //bde gshegs thugs rje can gyi zham ring byed //de'i rjes la bskald pa bzang po'i //blo gros chen po ldan ba ci snyed pa'i //sangs rgyas stong du bzhis ma tshang ba dag //de 
25b6(1): dag gis kyang byams pa mdun tu bdard //gang sngon rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer 'dir //bskald pa dag la rgyal ba mtha' yas mchod //zla ba mdzes pa'i 'od 'dra lha'i lus //zla 'od dpa' bo yang ni mdun na 'dug //snying rje mchog gi g.yon phyog+sa 
25b7(1): 'dug pa ni //'jam dpal bye ba brgya phrag mang po dang //dpa' bo rdzu 'phrul stobs kyis mngon 'phags pa //brgya phrag mtha' yas zhing du 'gro ba'o //de'i 'og tu nyan thos dge 'dun te //sha ri'i bu 'og me 'u dgal nag po dang //'od srung rab 'byord dga' 
25b8(1): bo ka pi na //bzang ldan rgyal po de bzhin ko'u di n+ya //kun dga' de bzhin gnas brtan sgra gcan zin //legs 'ongs kron ci gang po 'char ba po //nam gru gsung cen nyer 'khor ma 'gags pa //ral pa can ni stong du tshang ba dag //nyan thos dge 'dun 
25b9(1): 'og tu thams cad kyang //dka' thub drag mchog rig byed cho ga shes //dmod pa 'dor dang phan 'dogs nus pa yi //drang srong dul ba thal sbyord 'dud par byed //zhi ba'i yid dang dul ba'i yid rnams kyis //sangs rgyas 'jig rten sgron ma mdund 
25b10(1): bdard te //thugs rjer ldan ba'i myi'i seng ge gshegs //sems can stong rnams rab tu thard par mdzad //ston zla tha chungs zla ba nya ba ltar //skar tshogs dbang po yongs su dag pa bzhin //tshangs pa stong phrag dag ni brgya mang gis //
25b11(1): mtshungs pa myed pa'i stond pa yongs su bskord //grong khyer gyi ni sgo steng zhabs bzhag ste //nags tshal stug por bcas pa'i sa yang g.yos //lha dang lha myin lto 'phye'i tshogs rnams kyang //me tog bzang po dri mchog 'thor bar byed //
27a1(1): /myed //rgyal ba grong gshegs sangs rgyas rnam mdzes pa //de tshe 'od rnams thams cad yong myi mngon //tshogs cen dang ni bder gshegs lam bzhud de //gang tu stobs bcu yi ni zhabs 'jog par //sa la 
27a2(1): pad ma dag ni stong phrag brgya //mdab ma bye ba stong ldan gtsang ma byung //'drend pa grong khyer mchog tu gshegs pa'i tshe //de la nyal nyil myi gtsang myed par gyurd //grong khyer thams cad dri mchog gdug pa yi //spos ngad yid du 'ong 
27a3(1): ba kund nas ldang //grong dang srang rnams yongs su dag gyurd te //rdo dang gseg ma myed cing spos chus btab //stobs bcu ldan ba'i bsod nams de 'dra ste //rnam pa sna tshogs me tog sil ma bkram //gnod sbyin brgya stong gtum por 
27a4(1): sems pa dag //sangs rgyas myi dbang gser 'dra mthong bas ni //'dren la dga' ba rgya che rab bskyed nas //sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun skyabs su 'gro //lha rnams bye ba dang ni brgya phrag stong //myi dbang lta bar 'ongs pa de dag kun //bde bar 
27a5(1): gshegs la me tog char phab ste //me tog gtord nas nam mkha'i dkyil na 'dug //rgyal la myi yis me tog gang gtord pa //nam mkha'i ngos la me tog gdugs su gyurd //lha rnams kyis ni me tog gtord pa yang //sa steng lha'i me tog bkram
27a6(1): bar gyurd //lha dang myi dang grul bum srin po rnams //de mthong nas ni nam yang ngoms myi myong //mgon po stobs bcu ldan ba mthong nas kyang //rab tu dga' zhing sems bde rangs par gyurd //nam tshe skyes bu mchog gi sku mthong nas //
27a7(1): sems can thams cad dga' mgu rangs gyurd pas //lha'i me tog la yang yid myi sems //de dag la ni ngo mtshard 'dzind pa myed //stobs bcu'i g.yas phyogs na ni tshangs pa ste //de bzhin g.yon na brgya byin lha'i rgyal //nam mkha'i dkyil 
27a8(1): na lha mangs bye 'khod de //skyes bu mchog la shind tu gus pa skyed //lha dang lha myin dag gis rgyal ba bskord //lha dang myi'i dpal gzi kun phrogs nas //zhabs mthil sa la ri mo 'byind bzhin du //grong khyer du ni bcom ldan spyan drangs 
27a9(1): gshegs //sku lus dpe' byad bzang pos rgyas pa ni //nam mkha' skar ma dag gis gang ba bzhin //nam mkha' la ni zla ba nya ba ltar //rgyal po'i khab na sangs rgyas lam me bzhugs //nor bu rin cen rnam dag bzang po ni //nyes pa'i 
27a10(1): skyon bral rab tu snang bar byed //phyogs dang phyogs mtshams su yang 'od gtsang 'byung //de bzhin rgyal ba 'jig rtend kun snang mdzad //lha dang lha myin dgas gis rgyal ba bskord /myi'i gtso bo rgyal po'i khab du gshegs //zhabs 
27a11(1): mthil sa la ri mo 'byinadabazhin du //zla 'od gzho nu'i khyim du sangs rgyas gshegs //grong khyer mchog ni kund tu legs brgyand te //rgyal mtshan bye ba phrag stong rab tu bsgreng //de'i sa kun spos bzang rnam par byugs //yid 'ong 
27b1(1): de bzhin dbyard kyi me tog gtord //gang tshe bder gshegs gtam rnams stond pa na //mgon po srang bzhud myi la thugs brtse'i phyir //rgyal bas der ni 'phrul ba rnam spruld te //de dag la yang sangs rgyas chos bzang stond //
27b2(1): srog chags brgya stong dag gis de thos nas //sangs rgyas ye shes mchog la smon lam 'debs //nam bdag 'di 'dra'i ye shes thob snyam ste //bsam ba mkhyend nas rgyal ba lung yang stond //gang gis rgyal ba myi dbang spyan 'dras pa'i //
27b3(1): sbyin ba de ni mu mtha' myed gyurd te //rnyed mchog bsam gyis myi khyab des rnyed ces //la la dag ni de la mos pa skyed //'khor ba dag na shind tu mthong dka' ba //skyed dgu dag la phan mdzad thugs kyang brtse //thugs rje can de bdag gis sang 
27b4(1): la yang //spyan drang snyam du la la sems kyang skyed //la la ba gam mchog gi steng 'dug cing //lus ni shind tu sdug cing mdzes par brgyand //byang chub sems mchog dam pa bskyed nas ni //stobs bcu ldan la lha'i me tog 'thor //la las 
27b5(1): bla myed sems mchog rab bskyed nas //me tog tsam pa mdzes pa'i 'phreng ba dang //a ti mug ta de bzhin dbyard kyi spos //dar gyi lda ldi dag kyang la la 'thor //kha cig gos kyis shind tu lus brgyand te //me tog bzang po dag kyang thogs 
27b6(1): shing 'greng //me tog sna tshogs dar gyi lda ldi thogs //rgyal ba mthu' cen de'i phyogs su 'thor //la la pad ma dang ni ku mu da //ud pal gser gyi me tog bzang po 'thor //la la rin cen me tog de 'thor te //la la tsan dan phye ma 'thor 
27b7(1): bar byed //'drend pa grong khyer mchog tu gshegs pa'i tshe //ngo mtshard dpag tu myed pa rnams kyang 'byung //mtshungs myed de dag brjod par myi nus te //sangs rgyas ye shes skye bo bye mangs bkod //myi che myi gdung bden ba mthong ba dang //
27b8(1): gzhan yang gya nom snang dang shind tu mthong //de bzhin 'og myin 'dod tshags bral ba rnams //thams cad myi dbang lta phyir nye bar lhags //de bzhin dge ba'i lha rnams dpag myed dang //dge chung lha gzhan dag kyang sems dga' zhing //
27b9(1): dge rgyas khrag khrig dpag tu myed pa rnams //'drend pa drang srong chen po lta phyir lhags //tshad myed 'od kyang de bzhin dpag myed dang //'od chung gi ni lha rnams ci yod dang //'od gsal ba yang khrag khrig mang po der //'jig rtend mgon po 
27b10(1): lha phyir de dag lhags //tshangs 'khor pa dag brgya stong mang po dang //tshangs pa'i mdun 'dond dad pa de bzhin te //tshangs ris kyang ni brgya phrag mang po rnams //thams cad 'drend pa lta phyir nye bar lhags //de bzhin gzhan 'phrul gyi yang 
27b11(1): lha rnams dang //gzhan yang 'phrul dga'i sems can dag pa dang //dga' ldan 'thab bral lha rnams rab dga' zhing //thams cad sangs rgyas phyag 'tshal nye bar lhags //brgya byin lha rgyal sum bcu rtsa gsum pa //lha'i bu mo bye ba brgya 
28a1(1): //dang lhags //'dir ni me tog char yang rab 'bebs shing //sangs rgyas thub dbang lta phyir nye bar lhags //phyogs bzhi'i 'jig rtend skyong ba bzhi po yang //rnam thos bu dang klu dbang myig myi bzang //yul 'khor 
28a2(1): srung dang 'phags skyes sems dga' ste //de kun myi dbang stod cing nye bar lhags //gnod sbyin rgyal po mthu' ldan ir bir yang //dga' ba skyes shing gnod sbyin brgyas bskord te //nam mkha'i dkyil na 'dug cing sems dga' 
28a3(1): nas //me tog sna tshogs du ma'i char yang 'bebs //rtag tu myos dang 'phreng 'dzind lha rnams kyang //spos dang 'phreng ba rnam pa sna tshogs thogs //'khor dang bcas pa thams cad sems dga' shing //de ni skyes bu mchog la mchod pa 
28a4(1): byed //gnod sbyin lag na gzhong thogs mang po yang //gnod sbyin bu mo de dag rab brgyand te //gnod sbyin rol mo rab tu snyand pa dang //shind tu yid 'ong brgyas ni sangs rgyas mchod //glu dbyangs sil snyand 'jam zhing snyand 
28a5(1): pa la //mkhas pa'i myi 'am ci'i bu mo stong //myi 'am ci'i rgyal po ljond pa dang //rgyal mchog mchod par spos ngad ldang las lhags //bde' mchog spos ldan sgra gcan thag bzangs ris //lha myin bu mo stong gi 'khord 
28a6(1): bcas dang //lha myin rdzu 'phrul chen po gzhan mang yang //rin po che'i char phab nye bar lhags //srin po khrag khrig brgya phrag mtha' yas kyang //srin po bye ba brgya yis bsnyend bkur zhing //me tog sil ma sna tshogs mang po yang //
28a7(1): skyes bu mchog la gus par rab tu 'thor //glu'i rgyal po ri bo legs pa yang //'khor mang de bzhin gshegs pa'i drung du lhags //rin cen me tog rnam mang mchog thogs te //bde gshegs zhabs gnyis la ni rab tu 'dud //klu dbang pad ma dang 
28a8(1): ni pad ma che //de bzhin nor yod mtha' yas gdengs ka can //de dag khyu mchog drung du nyer lhags te //bde bar gshegs la gus par rab tu 'dud //klu'i rgyal po nor bu yid dga' zhing //khyu mchog zhabs kyi drung du nye bar 'ongs //klu'i me tog 
28a9(1): dri zhim mchog thogs te //ha cang myi ring bde gshegs spyan sngar 'dug //de bzhin ma dros klu'i rgyal po yang //klu'i bu mo shind tu bslabs pa'i mchog //rnga dang sil snyan stong phrag brgya rdung zhing //'jig rtend mgon po mchod phyir nye bar lhags //ma 
28a10(1): dros pa'i bu mo lnga brgya dag //ye shes yangs pa bla myed tshol ba'i phyir //gnyen mdab dag gis bskord cing dgar gyurd nas //rang gis rang byung mchog phyir nye bar lhags //de bzhin klu'i rgyal po sog myed kyang //skyes bu mchog la thal sbyar rab btud 
28a11(1): nas //klu'i mu tig mdzes shing bzang ba thogs //mkhal 'dug nas thub rgyal la mchod pa byed //de bzhin klu'i mu tig bzang zung yang //rab tu yid bde dga' mgu skyes nas ni //rin cen mu tig sna tshogs thogs nas su //der 'ongs 'drend 
28b1(1): pa la yang mthon bar 'thor //de bzhin klu'i rgyal po dus can yang //de bzhin gshegs pa'i drung du nyer 'ongs te //dga' zhing rin cen 'phreng ba mdzes mchog thogs //skyes bu dam pa la yang mchod rab byas //de nas bde gshegs 
28b2(1): yon tan rjes dran zhing //gus pa chen po shind tu bskyed nas ni //gnyen mdab klu mang dag gis de bskord cing //'drend pa'i bsngags pa rnam pa mang po rjod //de bzhin klu dbang dga' bo nye dga' dang //de bzhin 'jog po 'ge'u 
28b3(1): tam nag po yang //rgyal ba lta zhing der ni nye bar 'ongs //bde bar gshegs pa'i zhabs la 'dud pa byed //klu'i rgyal po ai la'i mdab ma yang //thub mchog rgyal ba'i 'od srung dran ba dang //bdag nyid myi khom skyes pa mthong nas ni //
28b4(1): klu brgyas yongs su bskord te ngu zhing 'ongs //kye ma bdag sngon the tsom byung gyurd te //bdag gis e la mdab stong sngon bcad pas //des ni myi khom bar yang bdag skyes te //rgyal ba'i chos ni rig par slab pa myin //shind tu smad 
28b5(1): pa skye bo'i lus 'di dang //klu'i skye gnas bdag gis rab spangs la //chos gang byang chub snying por skyes mchog gis //rtogs pa bsil ba bdag gis rtogs par bya //klu'i rgyal po rgya mtsho' 'khor los sgyur //bye ba gsum snyed klu'i 'khor 
28b6(1): kyis skord //chu lha gzi can rin cen do shal thogs //bcom ldan 'das la machod phyir nye bar 'ongs //rgyal ba la ni rdo 'phangs des bzung ste //de tshe nam mkha' yang 'dug gyurd pa //rgyal po'i khab kyi gnod sbyin ci zhig 'dzind //
28b7(1): bde bar gshegs pa'i spyan sngar gus par 'dug //lcang lo can gyi rgyal po'i gnas kund kyang //stong par gyurd cing gnod sbyin gang yang myed //thams cad kyis kyang phan tshun dam bcas te //'jig rtend mgon po lta bar lhags //de bzhin bong 
28b8(1): rna dang ni khab spu dang //gnod sbyin 'brog gnas 'jigs byed de bzhin te //gang gnas ring 'dug gnod sbyin bong bu dag //rgyal ba rang byung drung du nye bar lhags //dbang po'i tog dang gzugs bzang rad rod can //ba ku la dang rnam 'phel 
28b9(1): lnga po dang //gnod sbyin dbang po gzhan yang stong rnams ni //bdug spos snod rnams thogs shing nye bar lhags //lus dbyibs ma legs myi sdug mang po rnams //gzugs kyi rnam pa mang zhing rgyan bor ba //de'i dus na brgya stong mang po 
28b10(1): dag //gnod sbyin gyi ni me tog thogs te lhags //'ge'u ta ma ri byi phur du rgyal byed dang //ko'u shi ka dang 'od srung ma ra ka na dang //thams cad bshes dang par sha gar ga dag //thams cad sangs rgyas phyag 'tshal nye bar lhags //myi 
28b11(1): sbyin yang ni de bzhin dga' mgu ste //drang srong rgyas pa kud tsa ang gi ra //shed can ngan spong bad tsa gnas mchog rnams //de dag sangs rgyas phyag 'tshal nye par lhags //'dza ma na ba man be'i sham pa ya dang //'dza mag ni dang 
29a1(1): //grog mkhar drang srong mchog //dka' bar gnas dang myi'u thung spend pa rnams //'drend pa lta zhing grong khyer mchog tu lhags //drang srong mthong nas de tshe drang srong dga' //thub rgyal zhabs mchog la ni phyag 'tshald cing 
29a2(1): 'jig rtend sgrol la phyag byas mngon bstod de //thal mo sbyard nas dpa' bo'i spyan sngar 'greng //'jig rtend ci yod drang srong mang po kun //de dag myi dbang lta bar nyer lhags te //mthong nas mchod pa rgya cher byas nas kyang //
29a3(1): sangs rgyas spyan sngar thal mo rab sbyar 'dug //rgya mtsho' dag na gnas pa'i nam mkha' lding //bram ze'i cha byad du yang sprul byas nas //cod pan sna tshogs blta na sdug pa thogs //bde bar gshegs la phyag 'tshal nam mkhar 
29a4(1): 'khod //grong brgya dang ni 'dzam gling nags tshal na //gnas shing rtend pa'i lha rnams gang yod dang //grong gi lha mo ma lus thams cad kyang //nye bar lhags shing gtso bo mchod pa byed //nags tshal gyi yang lha rnams mtha' yas 
29a5(1): dang //ri'i lha rnams thams cad nye bar 'ongs //'bab chu'i lha kun mtha' dag de bzhin du //'drend pa mchod pa byed cing nye bar lhags //mya ngam dgon pa'i lha mo brgya rnams dang //ri rtse'i lha mo ci snyed ma lus dang //mtsho dang 
29a6(1): chu myig rdzing gi lha rnams dang //rgya mtsho'i lha yang sangs rgyas drung du lhags //lha dang lha myin klu dang gnod sbyin tshogs //myi 'am ci dang grul bum na ma mkha' lding //de bzhin yi dgas srul po mang po dag //skyes bu mchog 
29a7(1): la ri mor bya ba byed //'drend pa grong khyer mchog der gshegs pa na //rgyal ba mchog la de dag mchod pa byed //lha dang lha myin klu dag gnod sbyin rgyal //lta bas rtag tu chog par yong myi 'gyur //sngon gyi srid par 'jig rtend mgon po yis //
29a8(1): sngon gyi rgyal ba dag la mchod mchog byas //bsod nams 'bras bu sbyind pa de 'dra ste //skyes bu myi dbang lta bas ngoms myi myong //rgyal bas sangs rgyas zhing du 'od btang tshe //lhun po de bzhin rab lhun 'khor yug ri //kha ba can gyi ri dang 
29a9(1): spos ngad ldang //de dag rgyal ba'i 'od la sgrib pa myed //sangs rgyas zhing 'di rgya mtsho' ci yod pa //de dag de tshe sa dang 'dra bar gyurd //sangs rgyas zhing 'dithams cad kund tu yang //me tog dag gis gtord cing mnyam bar gyurd //'od 
29a10(1): gzer brgya stong dpag tu myed pa dag //chos kyi rgyal po'i zhabs kyi mthil nas byung //sems can dmyal ba thams cad bsil bar gyurd //sdug bsngal myed par byas nas bde ba myong //der ni stobs bcu ldan bas chos bshad pas //
29a11(1): lha dang myi'i myig kyang rnam par dag //srog chags brgya stong dpag tu myed par rnams //thams cad sangs rgyas ye shes nges par gyurd //'drend pa grong khyer mchog tu gshegs pa na //bder gshegs cho 'phrul mang po bdag gis ni //
29b1(1): bskald pa bye bar brjod du myi sla ste //rgyal ba 'der gshegs 'gro ba thams cad dga' //bder gshegs yon tan de dag dpag myed de //myi mchog yon tan rab kyi pha rold phyind //yon tan khyad par kund kyi pha rold 
29b2(1): sond //sangs rgyas bsod nams zhing la mgo'as phyag 'tshal ////grong khyer du gshegs pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o ////de nas bcom ldan 
29b3(1): 'das zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa'i khyim gyi srang du byung ste //zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa' khyim du gshegs so //gshegs nas gdan bshams pa la bzhugs so //byang chub sems dpa'i dge' 'dun dang dge slong gi dge 
29b4(1): 'dun yang so so gar 'os pa'i stan la 'khod do //de nas zla 'od gzho nur gyur pas bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun dang /dge slong gi dge' 'dun bzhugs par rig nas mchod yon chen po rims kyis
29b5(1): gsold te //zhal zas ro brgya dang ldan ba bzang zhing mang ba'i bza' ba dang /bca' ba dang bldag pa dang /bzhib pa dang btung ba rnams bdag nyid kyis gsold cing btard te //bcom ldan 'das bshos gsol nas lhung 
29b6(1): bzed bzhag ste /phyag bcabs par rig nas ras bcos bu phrugs bye ba stong phrag brgya ri ba bcom ldan 'das la gsold to //byang chub sems da pa' de dag dang dge slong gi dge 'dun la yang so sor chos gos sum phrugs 
29b7(1): phul nas stan las langs te /bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bzard nas /bcom ldan 'das la lha'i me tog man da ras gtord te /mchod cing mngon bar bstod nas /bcom ldan 'dasag la ba de logs su thal mo sbyard pa btud nas //
29b8(1): bcom ldan 'das la tshul dang 'dra ba'i tshigs su bcad pas mngon bar bstod do ///skyes pa myed cing 'gags myed pa //sku lus myi mnga' bsam myi khyab //gzugs dang mtshan ni phun sum tshogs //yon tan rgya mtsho' phyag 
29b9(1): 'tshal lo //dpa' bo shes rab mchog gis bskyed //thabs dang stobs kyi rtsald kyang mnga' //sangs rgyas nam mkha' 'dra ba ste //bzod mthar phyind la phyag 'tshal lo //dran ba nye bar bzhag pa bzhugs pa'i spyod yul te //
29b10(1): de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzi mal rab tu dga' dang bde //btung bar ting nge 'dzind te bshos su bsam gtan mchog //stong nyid dgond par gnas pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo //rgyal bas thugs rje bsnyend pas na //gzugs kyi sku 
29b11(1): yang stond par mdzad //sems can dag la thugs brtse ba'i phyir //btung dang bshos kyang gsol bar gda' ///e ma sangs rgyas bsam myi khyab //'jig rtend kund kyi 'drend pa ste //'jig rtend 'di ni khyod mchod 
30a1(1): //nas //mya ngan 'das pa mchog tu mchi //myi mnyam mnyam ba'i mgon gcig pa la de skad bstod //gzho nu yid kyang rab tu dgar gyurd tshig smras pa //sangs rgyas mtshungs myed bsam yas pa
30a2(1): bshos gsold pas //lha'i lha khyod bzhin du bdag kyang sangs rgyas gyurd ///de nas zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pas bcom ldan 'das la lha'i zhal zas ro brgya pa ni zhos par gsold //lha'i na bza' rin thang myed pa phugs
30a3(1): ni sku la gsold //lha'i me tog man da ras ni bcom ldan 'das la mchod //tshul dang 'dra ba'i tshigs su bcad pas ni mngon bar bstod nas //bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyard te //bcom ldan 'das
30a4(1): la yid kyis tshigs su bcad pas zhu ba zhus pa ///byang chub sems dpa' mkhas pas ci spyad na //rtag tu chos rnams rang bzhin rab tu 'tshal //mkhas pa bgyi ba dag la ci ltar 'jug //bgyi bar 'jug pa 'drend bas
30a5(1): bstand tu gsol //'drend pa skye ba dran bar ci ltar 'gyur //ci ltar mngal dag tu yang yong myi skye //ci lta bur na 'khor rnams myi phyed 'gyur //'di la ci ltar spobs pa mtha' yas 'gyur //sems can kun kyi spyod pa rab
30a6(1): tu mkhyend //chos rnams kun la mngon bar mkhyend pa 'jug //rkang gnyis gtso bo zilada' kyis myi non pa //zhu ba zhu yis bdag la lung bstan cig //chos kyi rang bzhin dngos myed rab mkhyend la //brjod du myed pa
30a7(1): tshig tu brjod par mdzad //seng ges '+wa rnams ci ltar pham bgyis pa //mu stegs gzhan la yang sangs rgyas de bzhin te //sems can kund kyi spyod pa rab mkhyend cing //chos rnams kun la ye shes rjes su 'jug //ye
30a8(1): shes chags myed spyod yul yongs su dag //chos kyi mnga' bdag bdag la de lung stond //'das pa mkhyend cing ma 'ongs de bzhin te //da ltar 'di na ci mchis pa yang mkhyend //dus gsum la ni ye shes thogs myed 'jug
30a9(1): de phyir bdag zhu shag kya seng ge la //dus gsum ldan ba'i rgyal ba'i chos dang ni //chos kyi rgyal pos chos nyid rab tu mkhyend //rang byang chos kyi ngo bo nyid la mkhas //de phyir bdag zhu ye shes rgya mtsho la //khyod la 'khrul
30a10(1): pa'i chos ni ci yang myed //de bas khyod kyi sems pa ma lus spangs //bdud spangs tha ba nyi mug rab tu bcad //dbang po bsod pa spyod pa stond //chos kyi mtshan nyid ci 'drar rgyal ba mkhyend //chos kyi mtshan
30a11(1): nyid de 'drar bdag la stond //bdag gis mtshan nyid de 'dra'i chos rtogs nas //mtshan nyid de 'dra'i byang chub spyod pa spyad //sems pa mtha' yas pa //ci ltar spyod pa spyad par 'jug pa lags //gang
30b1(1): tu sems can spyod pa 'tshal 'byung ba //spyod la 'jug pa bdag la bshad du gsol //mtshan nyid myi 'thun chos kyi rang bzhin mtshan //ngo bo nyid stong rang bzhin rnam dag pa //byang chub sems dpas ci ltar mngon sum 'gyur //
30b2(1): chos kyi tshul rnams bdag la bstand tu gsol //'di la chos rnams kund kyi pha rold phyind //bstand pa'i tshig kun la yang shind tu mkhas //the tsom myi mnga' nem nur the tsom chod //bdag la sangs rgyas byang chub bstand tu gsol /
30b3(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa'i sems kyi rtog pa thugs kyis mkhyend nas //zla 'od gzho nur gyurd pa la bka' stsald pa /gzho nu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos gcig dang ldan na /yon tan 'di
30b4(1): dag rab tu 'thob ste /myur du bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyang mngon bar rdzogs par 'tshang rgya'o //chos gcig po gang zhe na /gzho nu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid shes pa
30b5(1): yin no //gzho nu ci ltar na byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po chos thams chad kyi ngo bo nyid shes she na /gzho nu 'di la bya chub sems dpa' sems pa'i chen pos chos thams cad mying myed cing mying dang bral bar rab tu shes so /
30b6(1): chos thams cad sgra myed pa'i tshig gi lam dang bral ba //yi ge dang bral ba /skye ba dang bral ba /'gog pa dang bral ba /rgyu dang mtshan nyid myi 'thun ba /rkyend dang mtshan nyid myi 'thun pa /dben ba'i mtshan nyid 'di lta ste
30b7(1): mtshan nyid myed par mtshan nyid gcig pa /mtshan ma dang bral ba /bsam gyis myi khyab pa bsam ba dang bral ba /yid dang bral bar rab tu she so //de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so //
30b8(1): chos rnams bstand pa gcig po ni //chos rnams thams cad mtshan nyid myed //yang dag ci bzhin rab mkhyend pas //shes rab mchog gis bshad pa yin //de lta bur ni chos bstand rnams //byang chub sems dpa' sus
30b9(1): shes pa //mdo sde bye ba rab stond kyang //de la byang chub rgyun chad myed //'drend pas byind kyis rlabs pas na //yang dag nyid kyi mtha' yang shes //yang dag mtha' de rab shes na //de la ci yang bshad pa myed //
30b10(1): gcig gis kyang ni thams cad shes //gcig gis kyang ni thams chad mthong //ci snyed mang por bshad na yang //de la dregs pa skye ba myed //chos rnams thams can bdag myed par //de ltar sems kyis nges pa
30b11(1): brtags shing //mying du bstand pa bslabs pas na //yang dag nyad du tshig kyang smra //sgra dbyangs gang dag ci thos pa //de'i sngon gyi mtha' shes te //sgra'i sngon gyi mtha' shes nas //sgra dag gis ni de myi 'a
31a1(1): //sgra'i sngon mtha' ci 'dra bar //chos rnams mtshan nyid de bzhin te //chos rnams de ltar rab shes na //mngal du skye bar myi 'gyur ro //chos rnams thams cad myi skye zhing //'byung ba myed par rab tu shes //bstand 
31a2(1): pa 'di ni rab shes na //rtag tu skye ba dran bar 'gyur //nam zhig skye ba dran gyurd na //de tshe bya ba dag la 'jug //bya ba dag la zhugs pa yi //'khor rnams shind tu myi phyed 'gyur //de ltar chos rnams stong par ni //byang chub 
31a3(1): sems dpa' sus shes pa //des ni gang yang myi shes myed //'di ni ci yang myed pa'i mtha' //ci yang myed pa'i mtha' la yang //byis ba dag gis ci dag brtags //bskald pa bye bar de dag des //yang dang yang du 'khor bar 
31a4(1): gyurd //ci ltar 'drend pas mkhyend pa bzhin //gal te de dag rtog rig na //de la sdug bsngal myi skye ste //ngan 'gro rnams su 'ang de myi 'gro //so so'i skye bo thams cad kyis //de ltar tshul 'di ma shes pas //gang 
31a5(1): tu chos rnams ma 'gag pa yi //'di 'dra'i chos rnams spong bar byed //chos kun rnyed pa myed par ni //chos kyi 'du shes gang gyur ba //de lta bu'i 'du shes de //'du shes de ltar rig par gyis //rnam par shes dang 'du shes su //
31a6(1): byis ba dag gis 'di brtags te //brtags pa yi ni chos rnams la //mkhas pa 'dir ni rmongs myi 'gyur //stong pa rnyog pa myed pa'i chos //'di ni mkhas pa'i sa yin te //'di la byis ba'i spyod yul myed //sangs 
31a7(1): rgyas sras kyi spyod yul lo //zhi ba' stong pa rab bstand pa //'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i sa //'di ni sangs rgyas sras spyod pa //sangs rgyas chos kyi rgyan yin no //'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' dag //bag chags 
31a8(1): rab tu spangs gyurd pa //de dag gzugs kyis myi 'phrogs te //sangs rgyas rigs la de dag gnas //chos rnams thams cad gnas myed de //de dag la ni gnas yod myin //gang gis de ltar gnas shes pa //de la byang chub rnyed 
31a9(1): myi dka' //sbyin dang tshul khrims thos dang bzod //gnyen bshes bzang po brtend byas nas //bya ba 'di dag rnam rig na //byang chub myur du de 'tshang rgya //de la lha klu rtag tu bkur bsti byed //gnod sbyin lha myin lto 'phye dri za dang 
31a10(1): nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po thams cad dang //myi 'am ci dang srin po mchod pa byed //sangs rgyas bye ba de'i snyand pa rjod //bskald pa bye ba mang por myi chad par //chos bshad pa'i bsngags pa brjod na yang //de'i mu mtha' 
31a11(1): zad par dus myi 'gyur //byang chub sems dpa' gang gis stong shes pa //de ni srog chags bye ba'i don mang byed //shind tu des pa 'khor la chos stond te //thos nas dga' zhing ri mor byed pa skyed //de dag gis ni ye shes 
31b1(1): yangs pa 'byung //de phyir rgyal ba myi mchog mthong bar 'gyur //zhing rnams na yang bkod pa bzang po mthong //'jig rten mgon po de la chos rnams stond //chos kun sgyu ma lta bur shes par gyis //ci ltar bar snang rang bzhin stong 
31b2(1): pa ltar //de dag gi yang rang bzhin de 'drar shes //de ltar spyod pa ci la yang chags myi 'gyur //ye shes chags pa myed pas don byed cing //'jig rten na yang byang chub spyad mchog spyod //shes pas chos kun la yang rab brtags 
31b3(1): te //de dag zhing rnams gzhan du spruld pa 'gyed //spruld pa de dag sangs rgyas don byas nas //ci ltar chos nyid bzhin du rang bzhin 'gyur //byang chub sems la myi gag gnas pa rnams //bsam pa ci bzhin don rnams rnyed par 
31b4(1): 'gyur //sangs rgyas rigs ni gnas par gang brtson ba //de ni rtag tu sangs rgyas byas pa gzo' //rnam par 'bar ba'i lus dang rab ldan zhing //mtshan bzang sum bcu rtsa gnyis de la 'byung //phan yon gzhan yang mang po mtha' 
31b5(1): yas pa //ting 'dzind mchog la spyod pas thob par 'gyur //rtag tu myi sguld chos kyang spyod par 'gyur //de'i gzi brjid rgyal pos myi bzod de //mkhas pa su dag sangs rgyas chos spyod pa //byad bzhin bzang zhing shind tu mdzes par 
31b6(1): gyurd //bsod nams gzi' brjid dpal gyis mngon 'phags te //lhas kyang de'i gzi' brjid bzod myi 'gyur //su dag byang chub sems la brten gnas pa //de ni rtag tu srog chags kun kyi bshes //sangs rgyas byang chub 'di ni rab 
31b7(1): stond pa //de la nam yang mund pa myed par 'gyur //sgra dang tshig gi lam bral brjod du myed //nam mkha' ci bzhin chos kyi rang bzhin te //'di lta bu'i tshul mchog rnam rig na //de'i spobs pa dag kyang myi bzad 'gyur //mdo sde dag ni brgya 
31b8(1): stong rab bshad kyang //sngon gyi mtha' ma phra ba rab tu shes //chos kyi rang bzhin shind tu phra shes pas //mkhas pa'i tshig ni rtag tu thogs pa myed //rtag par tshul brgya dag la mkhas gyurd cing //sgra dang nges tshig rnam pa mang la mkhas //
31b9(1): las dang 'bras bu rnam smyin nges pa ste //khyad par 'phags pa'i bye brag de 'drar 'gyur //mkhas pa bdag nyid chen po stobs bcu'i sras //ma tshang myed pa'i stobs kyis shugs 'chang zhing //shind tu phra ba'i rang bzhin chos shes pas //
31b10(1): de'i dran ba rtag par yongs su dag //shind tu phra ba'i rangabazhin chos shes pas //yid du myi 'ong sgra ni thos myi 'gyur //rtag tu snyand cing yid 'ong sgra yang thos //de'i tshig ni rtag tu yid 'ong 'gyur //shind tu phra ba'i rangang bzhin chos 
31b11(1): shes pas //dran dang blo gros shes rab rtogs dang ldan //de bzhin sems kyang rnyog myed dang bar 'gyur //mdo sde brgya phrag du ma rab tu 'chad //yon tan 'di 'dra'i rang bzhin chos shes pas//yi ge dang ni tshig rnams 'byed la 
32a1(1): //mkhas //tha dad pa'i sgra mang du ma shes //don dang tshig 'bru la yang mkhas par 'gyur //shind tu phra ba'i rang bzhin chos shes pas //lha dang myi dang klu 'brug srin po dang //lha myin lto 'phye myi 'am ci la yang //rtag 
32a2(1): tu sdug cing yid du 'ong bar 'gyur //shind tu phra pa'i rang bzhin chos shes pas//'byung po yi dgas srin po'i tshigs rnams ni //sha la za ba shind tu myi bzad pa //de dag nam yang de la 'jigs myi skyed //mkhas pa de dag yang+sa 
32a3(1): pa'i gtam thos nas //shind tu dga' zhing ba spu zing zhes byed //sangs rgyas la yang dga' ba rgya cher skyed //de dag bsam gyis myi khyab don cen 'thob //bde bar gshegs pa'i chos mdzod 'di bzung ba //bskald pa stong mang 
32a4(1): de'i bsod nams stobs //rab tu brjod kyang rdzogs par myi nus te //dpag tu myed cing mtha' yas tshad myed do //ting 'dzind zhi ba'i mchog 'di bzung ba yis //'das pa'i rgyal ba dpag myed thams cad dang //sangs rgyas ma byond 
32a5(1): pa dang phyogs bcu na //sangs rgyas gang bzhugs de dag de yis mchod //skyes bu la la bsod nams 'dod pas 'dir //stobs bcu ldan ba'i thugs rje can dag la //dga' ba dpag tu myed pa bskyed nas ni //bskald pa mtha' 
32a6(1): yas dpag myed rim mgro byas //skyes bu gnyis po bsod nams 'dod pa yis //don dam tshul 'di las ni tshigs bcad gcig //phyi ma'i dus su gyurd tshe 'dzind pa ni //de la snga ma'i bsod nams char myi phyod //
32a7(1): 'di ni sangs rgyas mchod pa rab mchog ste //phyi ma myi bzad dus su gyurd pa'i tshe //'di las tshigs bzhi'i tshigs bcad gcig thos nas //'dzind pa la ni sangs rgyas thams cad mchod //de dag rtag tu rnyed mchog legs par rnyed //
32a8(1): de dag grong gi zas mchog legs par zos //stobs bcu'i sras kyi thu bo dam pa ste //de yis rgyal ba mang po yun ring mchod //bya rgod phung po'i ri 'dir nga yang mthong //de dag sangs rgyas mkhyend la nga lung bstand //mya ngan nga 'das nas ni 
32a9(1): byams pa yang //de tshe de la lung yang stond par 'gyur //de bzhin sangs rgyas tshe dpag myed pa yang //nga'i bde ba can du kun 'dong zhes //phan yon rnam pa du ma de la 'chad //mngon dga' der song sangs rgyas myi 'khrugs mthong //bskald 
32a10(1): pa brgya stong dpag tu myed par yang //log par lhung ba'i 'jigs pa yong myi 'byung //byang chub spyad pa mchog 'di spyod pa ni //rtag tu yid rab bde ba myong bar 'gyur //de phyir bye brag 'di 'phags de 'dra ste //'di ni rab tu bshad pa phan 
32a11(1): yon mchog //so so'i tshig la nga'i rjes slobs la //phyi ma'i dus la mdo sde 'di zung shig ////
32b1(1): mdo sde 'dzind pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o ////gzho nu de la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid shes pa de'i yon tan dang phan yon de lta bur 'gyur ro //de bzhin
32b2(1): gshegs pa'i yon tan dang bsngags pa yang dag par smra'o //de bzhin gshegs pa yang dag pa ma yin ba dang myed pas myi skurd to //de ci'i phyir zhe na /chos gang gis de bzhin gshegs pa rab tu dbye ba'i chos nyid de yang dag pa ci lta ba
32b3(1): bzhin du rab tu shes shing sangs rgyas kyi yon tan mtha' yas par rab tu shes so //de ci'i phyir zhe na /gzho nu sangs rgyas kyi yon tan ni mtha' yas te bsam gyis myi khyab cing bsam ba dang bral bas na //de dag bsam ba 'am dpag par myi nus so //
32b4(1): de ci'i phyir zhe na /gzho nu sems kyi ngo bo nyid ni gzugs myed pa bstan tu myed pa de gzho nu de ltar seMs kyi ngo bo nyid gang yin ba sangs rgyas kyi yon tan gyi ngo bo nyid kyang de yin no //sangs rgyas kyi yon tan gyi ri bo nyid gang yin ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i ngo bo nyid kyang de yin ni //de bzhin
32b5(1): gshegs pa'i ngo bo nyid gang yin ba chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid kyang de yin te /gzho nu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang de ltar yon tan thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid don gcig tu bstand pa yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes pa de
32b6(1): ni gzho nu byang chub sems dba' sems dpa' chen po yid kyis nges par brtags pa zhes bya ste //'byung ba la mkhas shing khams gsum las 'byung ba yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhig du rab tu shes so //ci lta ba bzhin du lta zhing log par myi
32b7(1): smra /rnam pa gzhan du myi smra /ci skad smra ba de bzhin byed /khams gsum pa la ma chags shing /khams gsum pa las yang dag par 'das te /'dod pa'i sa dang /gzugs kyi sa dang /gzugs myed pa'i sa dang /nyon mongs pa'i sa dang /mying gi sa dang /
32b8(1): sgra'i sa las 'das shing yi ge dang /chos kyi tshul la mkhas pa /ye shes yi bzang bar bsgoms pa /brjod du myed pa'i chos rtogs pa /yi ge shes pa yi ge la mkhas pa yi ge dang tshig rab tu dbye ba shes pa la mkhas pa yi ge dang chig rab tu dbye ba rgyas pa shes
32b9(1): pa la mkhas pa /chos thams cad rab tu dbye ba shes pa las mkhas pa /chos thams chad rab tu dbye ba rgyas pa zhes pa la mkhas pa /chos tha mas cad rnam par dgod pa la mkhas pa yin te /gtan la bab pa'i blo dang ldan zhing //bdud sdig
32b10(1): can dang /bdud kyi ris kyi lha rnams kyis zild kyis myi nond to //chos kyi rnam grangs 'di bshad pa'i tshe /lha dang myi'i skye dgu sngon yongs su sbyang ba byas pa bye ba khrag khrig phrag dgu bcu rtsa brgyad kyis bye ba brgya stong 'khyil pa'i gzungs dang /
32b11(1): sgrib pa myed pa dang /chos rnam par mthong ba'i bzod pa so sor thob nas /bcom ldan 'das kyis de dag thams cad dang /bskald pa grangs myed pa brgya stong phrag bzhi bcu rtsa brgyad ni //bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs
33a1(1): //par 'tshang rgya ste /thams cad kyang mying ni tha dad /tshe'i tshad ni gcig par sangs rgyas kyi zhing so sor bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon bar rdzogs par 'tshang rgya'o zhes lung bstand to ///de la 'di skad ces bya ste //
33a2(1): byang chub sems dpa' blo dang ldan ba gang //bla na myed pa'i byang chub rab tshol phyir //chos dang de bzhin don la mkhas pa de //chos kyi ngo bo nyid la spyod pa yin //de dag ma yin ba'i tshig myi smra //sangs rgyas yon tan bye brag ci 'dra ba //rgyal ba rnaMs 
33a3(1): kyi chos ni de yin te //dpa' bo the tshom bral bas shind tu rig //chos kun don gcig par ni rab shes te //mgo' gcig tu ni stong pa nyid yin bas //de dag la ni tha dad gyurd pa myed //don gcig la ni shind tu bslabs pa yin //rtog pa myed cing rnam 
33a4(1): rtog myed pa dang //dmyigs pa myed par blo gros ldan bas shes //zad pa dang ni myi zad 'du shes kyang //ma lus par ni thams cad rab tu spangs //de ni chos sku myi'i seng ge la //stobs bcu ldan ba gzugs su myi lta zhing //de ni phyind ci log 
33a5(1): kun rab spangs pas //mtshan rnams su yang lta bar yong myi 'gyur //chos rnams 'di dag bsam myi khyab //bsam dang bral zhing rang bzhin zhi //de lta bur ni rab shes na //rkang gnyis gtso bos sangs rgyas mthong //ci ltar bdag gi 'du shes 
33a6(1): rig //de bzhin kund tu blo yang gtong //chos kun de'i rang bzhin te //rnam par dag pa nam mkha' bzhin //yid la nams kyang 'byung ba myed //chos rnams thams cad shes pas na //khams gsum las ni rnam par grol //de la smon 
33a7(1): lam 'debs pa myed //yang dag ci bzhin du ni mthong gyurd cing //ma log tshig smra rnam pa gzhan myi smra //de'i tshig dang brjod pa thams cad kyang //rgyal ba dag gi mthu yis 'byung bar 'gyur //'dod pa'i sa dang nyon mongs sa rnams dang //
33a8(1): gzugs dang gzugs myed las kyang shind tu 'da' //chos rnams la ni yid kyang ma chags shing //rab tu dga' bas 'gro la phan bar spyad //mying gi sa las 'das shing sgra yang shes //ngo bo nyid kyis gsog tu gyurd pa ste //ci srid tsam 
33a9(1): du yun ring brjod na yang //de la gnas par byed pa yod ma yin //'du shes rab tu rgyu ba myed //lta ba log pa kun nas bstsald //blo yang shind tu gtan la bab //brtan ba nam mkha' 'dra bar spyod //gal te bdud bye khrag khrig rnams //
33a10(1): de'i sems dkrug phyir 'ongs kyang //bdud rnams thams cad zild mnand te //de'i dbang du 'gro myi 'gyur //bdud kyi rgya mo rnam spangs te //tshul khrims yongs dag gdung ba myed //bsam gtan dag gi bde la mos //'jig 
33a11(1): rtend stong bar rab tu shes //'jig rtend phung por smras pa yang //de dag stong par rab tu rig //skye ba myed cing 'gag myed pas //chos rnams thams cad nam mkha' bzhin //de dag bdag nyid gtong byed kyi //stobasa'

33b3(1): kyi khe ni rnam spongs la //skye bo mang la chos rab stond //sangs rgyas yon tan mang ldan ba //rang 'byung 'gyur bar gang 'dod pas //dge ba 'di la rab bslabs na //stobs bcu 'chang ba sangs rgyas 'gyur ////

33b11(1): su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so //ting 'dzind mnyam ba myed pa'i sa //shind tu mthong dka' phra zhing zhi //'du shes thams cad bcom ba ste //de phyir ting nge 'dzind ces bya //rtog pa myed cing rnam rtog myed //gzung ba myed cing bstand 
36a1(1): //'khod pa rnams //bcom ldan 'das kyis don ldan gsung thos nas //thams cad 'dren la rab tu dga' ba skyed //tshig ni rab tu stand cing bzang la 'thad //mnyen zhing 'jam la yid 'ong dus dang 'byord //gsung gi yan

36a3(1): ni zild kyis non //khu byug bya mang 'jam ba'i sgra 'byind dang //kun la shind tu yid 'thad dbyangs snyan dag //bya tshogs mang po thams cad sgra 'byind pa //sangs rgyas gsung gi char yang des myi phod //lha'i bu mo sgra dbyangs snyand pa dang //
36a4(1): sil snyand rol mo glu dbyangs 'jam ba dang //pi bang rdza rnga rnga cen dung gi sgra //de dag sangs rgyas gsung gi char myi phod //khu byug ne tso ri skegs sgra rnams dang //khrung khrung rma bya myi 'am ci'i sgra //sgra dbyangs yid 'ong snyand pa ci yod pa //

36a6(1): 'gyur yin //lha myi myi dbang lha ma yin rnams dang //srid gsum sems can mtha' dag ci yod pa //'od byed rnams kyi 'od snang de dag ni //bder gshegs 'od gzer gcig gis zild kyis non //bder gshegs sku ni me tog rgyas 
36a7(1): pa bzhin //mtshan rnams kund kyis sna tshogs spras shing brgyand //bsod nams brgyas grub dag cing rnam par dang //rgyal ba'i sku ni 'jig rtend kund tu lhan //dung gi sgra dang khar rnga sgra snyand dang //rnga'i sgra dang de bzhin kim pi la'i

36a10(1): sbyin lha myin lto phye dang //lha dbang tshangs pa lha'i bdag po'i sgra //srid gsum yid 'ong 'thad pa ci yod pa //de dag sangs rgyas dbyangs kyi char myi phod //tshangs pa'i 'od dang lha'i dbang po dang //nyi ma dang ni nor bu rin cen 'od 
36a11(1): kun kyi 'od ni rnam pa du ma dag //kund kyang rgyal ba'i 'od kyis zild kyis non //lus dang ngag dang yid kyang shind tu rnam par dag //ye shes dag pa srid pa gsum na gos pa myed //myi dbang yon tan dbyig tshogs yon tan rin

36b6(1): zhu ba lags //byang chub dam pa mchog 'dir rab tu tshol ba'i phyir //'drend pa bskald pa gang 'ga'i bye snyed dang //de las lhag par yang ni spyad pa rab tu spyad //gang gi don tu 'dzum ba 'di mdzad bstand tu gsol //ye shes dam pa

38b11(1): shind tu nges pa'i ye shes mchog gnas pas //pha rold rgol ba dag gis zil myi non //chags pa spangs shing rnam par grol la reg //dngos po rnam bshig srid par chags myi mdzad //khyod la spros pa mtha' dag kun myi mnga' //

64b4(1): glu dbyangs sbyard cing blangs nas su //zhum ba myed cing myi g.yel bar //sangs rgyas kyi ni ring bsrel mchod //byang chub mchog gi don kyi phyir //spos dang 'phreng bar bdug pa dang //gos dang phye ma ci snyed pa //de dag kund kyis
64b5(1): mgon mchod kyis //ting 'dzind zhi ba 'di tshol phyir //mchod pa ci snyed ci yod pa //dpag myed bsam gyis myi khyab pas //sangs rgyas kun la mchod par gyis //bla myed sangs rgyas ye shes te //chags myed ye shes

64b8(1): par byas //dam bcas pa las ma g.yo bar //khyod ni nan tan gnas par gyis //zad pa'i dus su nyer gnas tshe //gang gis mdo sde 'di bzung ste //rab tu brjod pa gzung byas na //myur du spobs pa thob par 'gyur //

65a10(1): sems dpa' snying rjes nond pa'i sa //nor dang 'bru dang rin cen btsun mo dang //sras dang sras mo rgyal srid rgyas pa dang //bdag gi sha yang gtong la yi rang ngo //shes rab yongs su bsgoms pas lus dul zhing //thabs la mkhas pa'i bslab pa rab 
65a11(1): bslabs pa //sems can dond du rab tu dga' sems kyis //bdag gi lus kyang gtong la yi rang ngo //gcig pu gnyis su myed par nags gnas shing //rtag tu bse ru ltar gyurd 'tsho ba gtsang //khor mor bya ba nyung zhing su dag ni //khe phyir

72a4(1): 'gro dang 'jug myed mkhas pa'i chos kyi 'gros //chos kyi dbyings ni gar yang myi 'gro ste //de 'dra'i 'gro ba 'gro myed chos kyi 'gros //dbyangs kyi tshogs myed ngo bo nyid kyi 'gros //'gro ba'i rang bzhin gang tu yang myi gnas te //myi gnas myi 
72a5(1): rtend rang bzhin 'jug pa ni //rgyal ba'i spyod yul rdul bral zhi ba'i gnas //zhi ba rab zhi nye bar zhi ba'i 'gros //'gro ba der ni gar yang myi gnas te //rtag tu dngos po'i rang bzhin rjes su song //mkhas shing myi g.yo' mthong dka' shind tu gnas //
72a6(1): de ni myi g.yo bdag nyid gnas pa ste //myi gnas ma 'ongs rang bzhin gnas pa yin //rang bzhin gnas pa bshad par myi nus te //de ni stong pa myi g.yo' chos kyi gnas //dbyangs kyis bshad kyang dbyangs kyi 'jug pa myed //dbyangs kyi rang bzhin
72a7(1): 'jug pa chos kyi 'gros //dbyangasakyi tshogs gnas gang na yang yod ma yin //de ltar rang bzhin 'jug pa chos kyi 'gros //'gros kyi sgra gsungs sems can 'gro ba myed //chos kyi rang bzhin mkhas don 'gro ba ste //dbyangs kyang brjod de sems 
72a8(1): can 'gro ba myed //dbyangs rnams myi rnyed sems can 'gro ba myed //mtha' dang mtha' myed ma yin dbus 'gro myed //yod myed ma yin phyogs su 'gro ba myed //rang bzhin 'gro ba ci 'dra shes pa ni //rgyal ba mchog gi bstand pa 'di dag 
72a9(1): mnyam //rdul bral rnam par dag pa don dam gnas //zhi zhing rab zhi rdul myed rdul bral ba //rtog dang rlom sems myed pa rab zhi gnas //rgyal ba thugs rje'i mchog ldan stond par mdzad //'di la yi ge spyod pa yod ma yin //'gro ba 
72a10(1): yangs shing yangs don 'gro ba ste //sangs rgyas dag gis bsnyend cing rgyal bas bstod //snang ba'i chos kyi tshul ni 'jug pa phra //de la gnas nas bde gshegs mtshungs myed pa //chos kyi gter ni rdul bral yangs pa dang //rin cen 
72a11(1): chos rnams rdul bral don dam stong //zhib pa'i don la 'jug pa stond par mdzad //rkang gnyis gtso bos ting 'dzind 'di bshad pa //rgyal po brtan byin gyis ni de tshe thos //de ni bye ba khrag khrig brgyad bcu dang //rgyal ba snying rje can 
72b1(1): drung rtag tu 'ongs //rgyal la stobs ldan gus pa rab skyed de //zhabs la myi'i bdag po phyag 'tshal nas //de tshe stobs bcu ldan ba'i spyan sngar 'dug //rab tu dga' zhing sor bcu thal mo sbyard //de'i spyod pa yongs su dag rig nas //dbang po 
72b2(1): dag la dbang gi pha rold phyind //myi mchog rgyal ba mos pa mkhyend pa yis //de la ting 'dzind dam pa 'di bshad do //rgyal po de ni don dam 'di thos tshe //'phags pa'i dga' ba mtshungs myed skyes nas su //gling bzhi dag kyang thams cad rnam spangs te //
72b3(1): 'dod pa bor nas khyim nas mngon bar byung //rgyal bar 'gyur zhes byang chub dond tu yang //nam tshe rgyal pos sa bor rab byung tshe //'dzam bu'i rgyal mtshan 'di'i myi kund kyang //'dod pa'i yon tan rnam spangs rab tu byung //de tshe stobs bcu'i yon tan 
72b4(1): rgya che ste //brtson yid dge slong pha dang dge slong ma //de tshe sa mos ma btab rtsi 'khrungs ste //lha rnams kyang ni g.yog dang bsnyend bkur byed //ngur smrig chos gos gsum dag 'byung bar 'gyurd //dra ba mnyam zhing drub legs dpe' myed 
72b5(1): la //dri ma myed cing rdul bral gser brgyand pa //sangs rgyas yon tan bsod nams stobs las byung //gzho nu ltos shig rgyal mchog de yang ni //sa rnams thams cad rnam spangs rab byung na //zad pa'i dus na sems can mang po 
72b6(1): dag //shind tu longs spyod chung yang khyim myi gtong //shing sgrog dang ni bcing ba chad pa mang //gshe zhing spyo ba sdug bsngal myi dga' dang //bla'i chad pa mang po bzod par byed //longs spyod chung rab khyim na bza' ba myed //shind tu 
72b7(1): tshe yang thung la nor yang myed //rab tu bag chung bsod nams stobs kyang myed //myi mkhas bzo'i gnas kyang myi shes te //dbul por gyurd cing khyim na nor yang myed //dri ma dag gzhan gyi bud myed chags //phrag dog byed cing shind tu btsam ba 
72b8(1): myed //nyon mongs chos ldan tshul la myi gnas kyang //nged ni sangs rgyas nyid du 'gyur zhes smra //gsug gis slu zhing bab col byed pa dag //nga ni phyug po 'jig rtend chos nor sbyin //gnod pa byed cing tshul 'chos sgyu ma dag //nged ni sangs rgyas nying 
72b9(1): du 'gyur zhes smra //gzhan dag gsad cing bcing dang gtse la dga' //tshul khrims 'chal pa ma rung zhe sdang yid //byas pa myi gzo' dben byed 'tsher bar gnas //btsun ba byang chub spyod pa stond ces smra //gang las des ni byang chub spyod thos pa //
72b10(1): de nyid la ni khong khro rab bskyed nas //mkhas pas 'khruld pa tshig 'ag thos na yang //de la myi snyand brgya dag sgrog par byed //gzho nu nga'i tshig 'di thos nas ni //de dag dang ni stend par ma byed cig //byang chub dam pa reg par 'dod na ni //
72b11(1): rmyi lam du yang de la yid ma rtond //sbyangs tshul bsnyungs pa yon tan du ma rjod //yon tan bskald pa mang po brgyar bshad kyang //de'i yon tan dag la myi gnas na //byang chub zhi ba mchog ni rtogs myi 'gyur //khor mor yang ni 
73a1(1): //dge zhing 'jam bar gyis //tshul khrims dag cing rtag tu dad pa'i yid //khor mor tshul khrims yongs su dag par gyis //ring por myi thogs ting 'dzind mchog thob 'byung //ngar rgyal ma byed tha ba ma skyed par //rtag tu 
73a2(1): yid ni yongs su dag par gyis //nga rgyal rgyags dang 'chab pa kun spongs la //ting 'dzind mchog 'di rab tu thob par gyis //rtag tu yon tan las ni rgyal ba dran //gser mchog mdog 'dra shind tu 'od mdzad pa //mtshan mo gnam 
73a3(1): las skar mas gang ba ltar //thub pa'i sku ni mtshand kyis yongs su rgyas //'chag cing 'dug dang 'greng zhing nyal ba na //myi gag thub pa'i zla ba dran byed pa //de'i mdun na rtag tu stond pa bzhugs //de ni rgya cher mya ngan 'das par 'gyur //
73a4(1): gdugs dang rgyal mtshan bla re ba dan mchog //phye ma byug pa mang po kun thogs la //rtag tu bde bar gshegs la mchod pa gyis //ring por myi thog ting 'dzind mchog 'di 'thob //spos mchog 'phreng ba me tog sdug pa dang //rol mo 
73a5(1): sil snyan mang po rab thogs la //rgyal ba'i mchod rten dag la mchod pa gyis //rings por myi thogs ting 'dzind mchog 'di thob //glu gar bzang po sna tshogs mang po dang //yid gtsang bzhad gda' rol mo bzang po dang //rtag par mar 
73a6(1): me'i 'phreng sdug bzang po yis //mtshungs pa myed la mchod bzang rab tu gyis //kha rnga dang ni sgra snyan rdza rnga brgya //rnga dang gling bu'i sgra mchog sna tshogs dang //sgra snyan rol mo'i yon tan sna tshogs kyis //dang ba'i yid ldan 'drend pa mchod
73a7(1): par gyis //sangs rgyas sku gzugs shind tu mdzes pa dag //rin cen byi dor legs par byas pa yang //blta na sdug cing shind tu bzang po gyis //ring por myi thos ting 'dzind mchog 'di 'thathob //de bzhin gser dag dang ni dngul 
73a8(1): dag dang //tsan dan gyi ni sku gzugs bzang po dag //mdzes shing shind tu blta na sdug pa gyis //ring por myi thogs ting 'dzin mchog 'di 'thob //'ji ba dang ni de bzhin rdo ba dang //shing dang ri mor bris pa'i sku gzugs dag //mdzes shing 
73a9(1): shind tu blta na sdug pa kyis //ring por myi thogasating 'dzind mchog 'di 'thob //rtag tu nags khrod dben ba bsnyend par gyis //grong dang grong khyer dga' ba nam spangs la //bse ru lta bur gnyis su myed par gyis //ring por myi thog 
73a10(1): ting 'dzind mchog 'di 'theb //nga ni chos rje khyod ni nga'i bu //nga'i ting 'dzind spyod pa rjes su slobs //nga ni de tshe phyogs su grags pa'i //myi dbang brtan bas byin zhes bya bar gyurd //ting 'dzind zhi ba'i mchog 'di tshol ba'i phyir //
73a11(1): ngas sngon sangs rgyas mtha' yas mchod pa byas //rtag par ngas ni tshul khrims rnam dag bsrungs //nga yis stobs bcu dag la ri mor byas //ting 'dzind zhi ba'i chog 'di tshol ba'i phyir //ngas sngon bu dang chung ma yongs btang ste //
73b1(1): mgo dang rka lag myi mchog bzang po byin//zhum ba'i sems su nam yang yong ma byas//ting 'dzind zhi ba'i mchog 'di tshol ba'i phyir//nor 'bru bran po bran mo brgya mang dang //rin cen mang po ngas ni yongs btang ste//slong ba mang po shind tu tshim
73b2(1): bar byas//ting 'dzind zhi bi'i mchog 'di tshol ba'i phyir //ngas sngon gser dang mu tig shel mang dang //dung dang man shel dang ni be du r+ya//nor bu gzugs gtsang byu ru 'bru rnams btang //ting 'dzind zhi ba'i mtshog 'di tshol ba'i phyir//

73b4(1): du kul mchog //gser gyis spras pa mang po yongs su btang //ting 'dzind zhiba'i mchog 'di tshol ba'i phyar//rta dang bal glang shing rta sna tshogs dang //bu dang tshung ma rab sdug ngas btang ste//yid myi bde ba nam yang ma byung ngo //ting 'dzind zhi ba'i
73b5(1): mchog 'di tshol ba'i phyir//tshol bas sdug bsngal nyon mongs gyurd pa yi//shind tu dbul ba'i myi dag ngas mthong nas//de dag ngas ni dbul ba myed par byas//ting 'dzind zhi ba'i mchog 'di tshol ba'i phyir //rta dang bal glang shing ta khrag khrig
73b6(1): dag //nor bu rin cen brgyand pa'i dra bas g.yogs//ngas sngon slong ba dag la sbyin ba byas//ting 'dzind zhi pa'i mchog 'di tshol ba'i phyir//ngas sngon bskyed mos tshal ni mang po dag//bye ba khrag khrig legs par brgyan byas te//yid kyang

73b8(1): zhi ba'i mchog 'di tshol ba 'i phyir//rin cen phung po ri rab mnyam ba dang //de bzhin gos dang rgyan rnams mang po yang //ngas sngon slong ba dag ma sbyin ba byas//ting 'dzinid zhi ba'i mchog 'di tsholaba'i phyir//shind tu dbul ba ngas ni phyug por byas//
73b9(1): rab tu nyon mongs gyurd pa ngas bskyabs te//sdug bsngal gtses pa mang po bde bar byas//ngas ni sa steng dbang phyug gyurd pa'i tshe//skye ba'i tshogs rnams sdug bsngal mthong nas ni//snying rje bskyed nas bde bar gyurd cig ces//de dag la ni
73b10(1): nga yis rgyal srid btang //gzho nu ngas ni ngo mtshar byas pa dang //bskald pa brgya mang dka' ba ci spyad pa//ngas ni bskald pa bye ba khrag khrig du//de dag bshad kyang zad par myi nus so//ting 'dzind zhi ba'i mchog 'di tshol ba'i phyir//
73b11(1): ngas ni ngo mtshar dka' ba spyad pa ni//bde bar gshegs pa'i spyod lam ma dad pa//myi rnams sems ni myos shing bslad par 'gyur//gzho nu khyod la shind tu bsgo bar bya//dad par gyis shig nga la spyi bden myed//bder gshegs brdzun gyi
74a1(1): //tshig ni myi gsung ste //thugs rje rgyal ba rtag tu bden bar gsung //ci ltar ting 'dzind mchog kyang thob gyurd cing //sems can khrag khrig sdug bsngal thar bya zhes //bskald pa mang por spyod pa 'di dag dang //gzhan yang ngas 
74a2(1): ni rnam pa mang po sbyangs //skad cig gang la ting 'dzind 'di yang ni //nga yis rab tu thob par gyurd pa'i tshe //ting 'dzind dam pa ye shes chen po'i lam //ngas thob sangs rgyas khrag khrig mang po mthong //rdzu 'phrul dag kyang mtha' yas ngas thob 
74a3(1): ste //zhing brgya dag tu rnam par spruld pas 'gro //song nas ngas ni snying rje ldan ba la //dri ba bye ba khrag khrig mang po dris //bder gshegs dag gis nga la ci bstand pa //de tshe dri ba bye ba khrag khrig bzung //nga yis ma lus thams cad bzung byas

74a5(1): nas su //bskald pa brgyar yang yang dag tshul bslabs te //ngas sngon sems can bye ba khrag khrig rnams //rdul dang bral ba'i lam mchog dam par bkod //gang gis sngon gyi bde bar gshegs pa dag //rdul dang bral ba'i tshul 'di stond pa rnaMs 
74a6(1): ma mthong bas ni don dam stong pa yi //ting 'dzind mchog la dad par myi nus so //myi gag dad cing mkhas la cho ga shes //yang dag tshul zab tshul rnams thob pa ni //de dag myi skrag dngang bar myi 'gyur ste //thos nas rtag tu yid kyang rangs

74a11(1): dug dang mtshond kyis myi tshugs te //dgra dag gis kyang brgal bar myi nus 'gyur //myi gag ting nge 'dzind mchog 'di 'dzind pa ni //de dag nags khrod shing gnas rtag par yang //lha dag khor mor bsnyend bkur byed par 'gyur //gnod 
74b1(1): sbyin mang po de'i g.yog kyang byed //myi gag ting 'dzind mchog 'di 'dzind pa ni //ye shes dag kyang rgya mtsho 'dra bar 'gyur //thub pa'i yon tan brjod la thogs pa myed //sangs rgyas yon tan yang dag rjod par byed //myi gag ting 'dzi nad mchog

74b3(1): 'khord kyi nang du dga' bar 'gyur ba stond //seng ge bzhin du de ni sgra rnams sgrog //myi gag ting 'dzind chog 'di 'dzind pa ni /de dag smand pa dpyad mkhan 'dra bar yang //mang po'i rtend dang dpung gnyen skyabs su 'gyur //'jig
